# Nabd Plus Phase 0 findings register — v1

Baseline: `22526bedb77a3d8148219036367e4714f401aecc`
Scope: source audit only; no remediation.

## Confirmed source findings

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-001 | P1 | Patient Web catch-all exports POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE but effective patient allowlist accepts GET only; mutation capability is therefore absent at this boundary. | `app/api/patient/[...path]/route.ts`; `lib/api/patient-allowlist.ts:46–48` | Decide and document read-only launch or implement each mutation as an explicit contract-tested BFF route; never widen catch-all implicitly. |
| F-002 | P1 | Mobile entry and tabs use `@ts-nocheck`, disabling type safety at auth/navigation roots. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/index.tsx:1`; `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx:1` | Remove or narrowly justify with tracked debt, with typecheck passing for these roots. |
| F-003 | P1 | Mobile `EmailAuthProvider.login` returns access and refresh tokens to the AuthResult caller and supplies fallback identity/role values. | `src/services/auth/providers/EmailAuthProvider.ts:16–34` | Confirm native session contract; remove misleading identity defaults and prove secure storage/propagation and logout revocation. |
| F-004 | P1 | Provider ContractModal falls back to hard-coded legal copy and closes after acceptance failure. | `NabdProvider-provider/src/components/ContractModal.tsx:59–88,113–122` | Legal copy must be server-approved/versioned; acceptance failure must block closure and expose retry. |
| F-005 | P1 | Provider DoctorDashboard has client fallbacks for queue/stats and uses legacy-looking job routes; insurance UI exposes patient/policy information. | `DoctorDashboard.tsx:189–419` | Bind to verified provider contracts, fail visibly on unavailable data, enforce role/ownership/PHI minimization and audit. |
| F-006 | P1 | Provider Nursing surfaces silently convert queue errors to empty state, use fixed distance/fallbacks and include SOS/refund claims needing contract proof. | `NursingDashboard.tsx:80–160,226–232,356–678`; `NursingFieldOps.tsx` | Surface errors distinctly, use server GPS/SOS/refund contracts, and test provider/stranger/unauth/state transitions. |
| F-007 | P1 | Patient Home-care service detail has no provider/address/slot/quote/payment/insurance-cash booking continuation. | `app/[locale]/home-care/services/[serviceId]/page.tsx:11–18` | Provide a complete server-backed journey or mark the capability blocked; no success placeholder. |
| F-008 | P1 | Patient medicine detail has no price/stock/prescription upload/cart/purchase continuation and is deliberately noindex because public entity contract is unreliable. | `app/[locale]/medicines/[medicineId]/page.tsx:16–36,40–64` | Approve public DTO/indexing contract and build real pharmacy continuation before enabling commerce/indexing. |
| F-009 | P1 | Patient prescriptions page is read-only: no detail, upload, renewal/reorder or pharmacy purchase CTA. | `app/[locale]/prescriptions/page.tsx:13–43` | Define prescription lifecycle and connect to verified upload/review/cart/order contracts. |
| F-010 | P1 | Patient Chat thread is read-only in the page and hides message bodies/attachments; send/upload/realtime behavior is not present there. | `app/[locale]/chat/[threadId]/page.tsx:11–12` | Decide supported chat scope and implement/test secure send/upload/realtime if required; verify PHI redaction end-to-end. |
| F-011 | P2 | Patient notification settings are read-only in the page; emergency is visually locked and no update mutation exists. | `app/[locale]/notifications/settings/page.tsx:18–31` | Define supported settings and provide contract-backed update or explicit blocked state. |
| F-012 | P2 | Family page is read-only; add/invite/remove/edit/member-switch actions are absent. | `app/[locale]/family/page.tsx:15–44` | Define family ownership/consent model and implement only approved actions. |
| F-013 | P2 | Insurance page is read-only; claim submission, eligibility/preauthorization and checkout insurance selection are absent. | `app/[locale]/insurance/page.tsx:14–38` | Define insurance decision flow and contracts for each service/payment branch. |
| F-014 | P2 | Diagnostics Labs and Radiology pages are catalog-only in the read source; there is no booking CTA; Radiology detail is explicitly blocked. | `diagnostics/labs/page.tsx:12–26`; `diagnostics/radiology/page.tsx:13–21` | Verify service detail/slot/quote/booking contracts, then enable only tested paths. |
| F-015 | P2 | Medicine detail links back to `/medicine-catalog` while the list source is `/medicines`, requiring route reconciliation. | `medicines/page.tsx:44`; `medicines/[medicineId]/page.tsx:61` | Establish one canonical route and test all locale links/redirects. |
| F-016 | P1 | Unified-bookings service acquires a Redis slot lock for 300 seconds (5 minutes), while the accepted contract/launch requirement specifies a 10-minute booking lock; release also deletes by provider/slot without validating the lock owner. | `audit-work/source/nabdah-backend/src/modules/unified-bookings/unified-bookings.service.ts:13–30`; `audit-work/source/nabdah-backend/src/modules/unified-bookings/unified-bookings.contract.spec.ts:94–98` | Reconcile one authoritative TTL, test concurrent owner/stranger/replay behavior, and release only the lock owned by the same patient/transaction. |
| F-017 | P1 | Mobile DiagnosticsHub exposes search and multiple filters but the confirmed source does not apply `searchQuery` or `activeFilter` to the rendered lists; API failures are converted to empty data, and lab cart lines carry client name/price. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/(tabs)/diagnostics.tsx:55–84,107–125,201–439` | Bind each control to a verified query/filter contract, distinguish unavailable from empty/no-match, and make server authoritative for price and availability before cart/checkout. |
| F-018 | P1 | Home-care compatibility transitions emit state events with swallowed emitter failures, while chat aliases expose send endpoints without idempotency/attachment/moderation evidence in the controller; Mobile nursing filters/actions are not mapped to these contracts. | `nabdah-backend/src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:116–138,274–308`; `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/(tabs)/nursing.tsx:80–103` | Prove durable event delivery/outbox, chat participant/rate-limit/moderation/idempotency controls, and complete Mobile/Web mapping to the canonical state machine. |
| F-019 | P1 | Home-care booking is JWT patient-only at creation, while patient UI exposes booking flows without an explicit auth/guest state in the confirmed source; the ownership and guest policy is unresolved. | `nabdah-backend/src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:21–23,74–95`; `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/(tabs)/nursing.tsx:129–145` | Decide guest policy and enforce consistent auth, ownership, cancellation/refund and post-booking access across Web/Mobile. |
| F-020 | P1 | Admin Master Dashboard substitutes synthetic panel coordinates for invalid/missing telemetry coordinates, silently logs fetch failures without visible retry, and falls back to localhost API base when environment is absent. | `web_admin_dashboard/src/pages/admin/dashboard.tsx:31–35,66–81,92–103,208–212` | Label visualization fallback as non-geographic or omit it, expose stale/unavailable/retry states, and fail closed on missing production API configuration. |
| F-021 | P0 | Admin Config Portal exposes global SLA and emergency kill-switch mutations with hard-coded `adminId`, localhost API fallback, and no page-level evidence of re-authentication, two-person approval, idempotency, server nonce or auditable change record. | `web_admin_dashboard/src/pages/admin/config-portal.tsx:18–99,167–210` | Verify backend authorization/audit/rollback; require server-derived identity, explicit confirmation controls, replay protection and tested fail-safe semantics before production. |
| F-022 | P1 | Admin Passkey UI claims mandatory 2FA, but the page does not prove the complete password+passkey enforcement, recovery, replay, role restriction or audit lifecycle; enrollment/removal calls show no explicit idempotency/re-auth in the page, and the enrollment button contains an emoji. | `web_admin_dashboard/src/pages/admin/security.tsx:20–77,81–88,116–125` | Verify backend/session enforcement and recovery with unauth/wrong-role/replay/last-key tests; require auditable re-authenticated mutations and comply with no-emoji UI policy. |
| F-023 | P0 | Provider NursingDashboard has route/payload drift against the backend compatibility contract (`/nursing/visits/.../respond` versus `/home-care/bookings/.../respond`, `{available}` versus `{online, available_now}`), silently empties failed queues, and presents hard-coded age/gender/distance fallbacks; quick actions may use the first job as context. | `NabdProvider-provider/src/screens/nursing/NursingDashboard.tsx:80–103,128–164,226–232,266–286,293–318`; `nabdah-backend/src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:98–113,141–149,209–212` | Reconcile exact route/method/body with live/backend controller, fail visibly on queue errors, remove fabricated operational/PHI values, and require explicit selected booking context for each action. |
| F-024 | P1 | Web medicine catalog is explicitly `noindex,nofollow` and has no add-to-cart/prescription/checkout CTA, while Web cart only reads `/cart` and renders totals without quantity/remove/checkout mutations; this is not parity with Mobile Pharmacy. | `nabd-patient-web/app/[locale]/medicine-catalog/page.tsx:19–29,32–44`; `nabd-patient-web/app/[locale]/cart/page.tsx:14–33`; `semantic-evidence-web-mobile-pharmacy-parity.md` | Decide legal/indexing scope, then implement only contract-backed commerce actions with server-authoritative price/stock/prescription/address and idempotency tests. |
| F-025 | P1 | Mobile Pharmacy stores server medicine responses in AsyncStorage and adds client-derived name/price/display fields to local cart; logout/account-switch invalidation, stale-price handling and server cart reconciliation are not proven in the read source. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/(tabs)/pharmacy.tsx:107–142,152–174` | Prove cache isolation/expiry, server revalidation and authoritative cart/checkout semantics; never trust client price or prescription flags. |
| F-026 | P1 | Provider Pharmacy navigator has a `home`/`orders` tab-state ambiguity and describes its broadcast feed as simulated WebSocket with polling fallback; the broad accept/partial/reject, prescription, inventory, delivery and wallet surfaces are not yet reconciled to exact role-protected contracts and idempotency/audit tests. | `NabdProvider-provider/src/screens/pharmacy/PharmacyDashboard.tsx:2–22,73–170,187–225` | Reconcile every operational action with backend method/schema/ownership, make realtime/polling truth explicit, and test duplicate broadcasts, financial mutations, PHI handling and provider authorization. |
| F-027 | P1 | Provider Radiology derives order/revenue state locally and resets failed inbox loads to an empty/zero dashboard, while displaying sensitive pregnancy/pacemaker/contrast questionnaire data and exposing report/catalog/schedule mutations whose clinical sign-off, media integrity, ownership and idempotency are not proven in the read source. | `NabdProvider-provider/src/screens/radiology/RadiologyDashboard.tsx:82–127,135–198`; `semantic-evidence-provider-radiology-dashboard.md` | Distinguish unavailable from empty, minimize and audit safety PHI, and prove exact role/state/report/media/payment contracts with replay and clinical approval tests. |
| F-028 | P1 | Backend user/settings mutations exist behind the patient Web/Mobile read-only boundary, but several use `body: any` and show no route-level idempotency or re-auth evidence for privacy/security/password changes, creating an unresolved contract and security mismatch. | `nabdah-backend/src/modules/users/users.controller.ts:19–21,34–36,50–58,66–88,91–99`; `semantic-evidence-backend-user-settings.md`; `nabd-patient-web/app/[locale]/settings/page.tsx:35–44`; `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/index.tsx:32–93` | Define typed DTOs/field allowlists, require re-auth where sensitive, standardize idempotency, prove owner/unauth/replay/session-revocation tests, and decide Web/Mobile mutation scope explicitly. |
| F-029 | P1 | Mobile Security Settings optimistically updates biometric/2FA preferences while swallowing PATCH failures, loads failed sessions as an empty list, posts password changes without visible re-auth/idempotency/policy controls, and may pass session `id` to a backend route defined with `jti`. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/security.tsx:39–116`; `nabdah-backend/src/modules/users/users.controller.ts:76–99`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-security-settings.md` | Add rollback/error distinction, reconcile `id` versus `jti`, enforce re-auth/password policy/idempotency, and test unauth/owner/stranger/replay/failed delivery/session revocation. |
| F-030 | P1 | Mobile Privacy Settings optimistically mutates privacy controls while swallowing failures, displays unverified ISO/encryption/no-sale claims, and submits personal-data deletion requests without visible idempotency, re-authentication, status lifecycle or duplicate-request handling. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/privacy.tsx:38–53,102–183`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-privacy-settings.md` | Prove approved legal/security claims, add rollback/error distinction and typed consent/deletion contracts, require identity assurance and idempotency, and test request status, duplicate/replay, cancellation and audit lifecycle. |
| F-031 | P1 | Mobile Notification Settings optimistically PATCHes notification preferences while swallowing failures, locks emergency notifications without a demonstrated server policy, and presents reminder timing/delivery claims without notification-service evidence or reduced-motion/accessibility handling. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/notifications-settings.tsx:21–32,47–64,98–145,204–278`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-notification-settings.md`; `nabdah-backend/src/modules/users/users.controller.ts:50–59` | Reconcile notification keys and emergency policy with typed backend contracts, add rollback/error/retry and delivery-status evidence, test idempotency and authorization, and provide reduced-motion/accessibility behavior. |
| F-032 | P1 | Mobile Notifications inbox performs individual read acknowledgement as fire-and-forget without visible idempotency or rollback, depends on an application route translation map for deep links, and renders notification body/title directly without demonstrated PHI/content policy. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/notifications/index.tsx:62–75,105–121,188–205`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-notifications-inbox.md` | Add tested idempotent read contracts and retry/error semantics, exhaustively validate route translation for all backend types, and enforce notification content minimization/ownership/audit rules. |
| F-033 | P1 | Mobile Data Management presents export, portability and deletion actions with empty callbacks while claiming JSON/PDF delivery, FHIR/HL7 compatibility and legal rights; storage failure/empty is rendered as loading and the storage denominator is hard-coded. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/data.tsx:18–63,84–150`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-data-management.md` | Replace claims with approved executable contracts or explicit blocked states, implement typed export/portability/deletion lifecycle with identity assurance/idempotency/audit, and distinguish loading/empty/unavailable with server-derived quotas. |
| F-034 | P2 | Mobile Language Settings changes global language locally but does not prove persistence, Web locale synchronization, RTL/layout reinitialization or six-locale completeness; every flag slot renders an empty string. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/settings/language.tsx:11–47`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-language-settings.md` | Verify all six locales and RTL behavior across restart/navigation, persist and synchronize locale policy, and either render approved vector flags or remove the empty slots. |
| F-035 | P1 | Mobile Profile exposes a guest sign-in/create-account CTA whose handler dispatches logout and navigates to welcome, rather than proving an authentication entry flow; it also exposes many route destinations with only partial guest gating and fallback display identity. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/profile/index.tsx:30–35,39–47,61–97,115`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-profile.md` | Reconcile CTA intent with auth navigation, verify every destination exists and enforces guest/owner policy, remove misleading identity fallbacks where appropriate, and test session/cache cleanup and unauthorized access. |
| F-036 | P1 | Mobile Addresses turns load failures into an empty list, exposes default-address PATCH without visible idempotency/identifier/ownership evidence, and renders an `إضافة عنوان جديد` button without a handler; edit/delete, validation, geocoding and delivery eligibility are absent from the source. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/profile/addresses.tsx:19–54,90–157`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-addresses.md` | Implement or explicitly block address creation/edit/delete, distinguish unavailable from empty, validate owner-scoped identifiers and idempotency, and prove address selection in cart/booking flows. |
| F-037 | P1 | Mobile Medical Profile Edit mutates sensitive health attributes and avatar in separate requests without visible idempotency/typed ownership controls; avatar upload can succeed before profile PATCH fails, leaving orphan media, while upload type/size and PHI handling policies are not proven. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/edit-profile.tsx:22–64,72–74`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-medical-profile-edit.md` | Prove server-side validation and patient ownership, secure media constraints/cleanup, idempotency and audit for health mutations, and test partial failure/duplicate/replay/unauth scenarios. |
| F-038 | P1 | Mobile Health Home swallows failures for multiple health/appointment reads, lacks explicit unavailable states, uses fallback appointment id `"1"` for waiting-room navigation, labels appointments as video without proving modality, and includes a literal emoji/reduced-motion gap. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/(tabs)/health.tsx:99–127,206–250,338–373`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-health-home.md` | Distinguish unavailable/empty states, reject fabricated identifiers and derive modality from server data, prove ownership before waiting-room navigation, remove unapproved emoji and test reduced-motion/accessibility behavior. |
| F-039 | P1 | Mobile Vitals Log posts manual readings with only presence checks and numeric coercion; finite/clinical-range/unit validation, typed DTOs, idempotency, duplicate/replay protection and ownership/audit evidence are not proven. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/vitals-log.tsx:9–16,18–52`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-vitals-log.md` | Enforce server and client validation for each vital type, use typed owner-scoped contracts, require idempotency/replay tests and audit trail, and verify invalid/duplicate/offline retry behavior. |
| F-040 | P2 | Mobile Medications calculates dose progress locally and labels a navigation action as “sync alerts” without proving device permission, push scheduling, dose-acknowledgement mutation, timezone semantics or server reconciliation; add/manage contracts are outside this screen. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/medications.tsx:21–32,37–55`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-medications.md` | Reconcile reminder/dose state with typed server contracts, verify device permission and notification delivery, define timezone/status semantics, and test duplicate/offline/replay and all reminder lifecycle states. |
| F-041 | P1 | Mobile Prescriptions logs load failures without a user-visible error/retry state, sends an unbound pharmacy CTA without a prescription identifier, shares medication names/doses through the device share sheet without demonstrated PHI consent policy, and displays unverified OCR accuracy claims. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/prescriptions.tsx:16–35,47–97`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-prescriptions.md` | Distinguish unavailable from empty, bind ordering to an owner-scoped prescription contract, require explicit PHI-sharing controls, and prove OCR provenance/confidence/correction and prescription access tests. |
| F-042 | P1 | Mobile Medication Reminder Add/Edit performs reminder mutation, notification-preference mutation and local scheduling as separate steps without visible idempotency, atomicity or compensation; timezone/DST, permission, taken/cancel/edit cleanup and cross-device synchronization are not proven. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/medication-reminder-add.tsx:16–61`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-medication-reminder-add.md` | Define an owner-scoped typed reminder contract with idempotent add/edit and atomic preference state, test partial failure/replay/offline behavior, and prove timezone, permission, cancellation and cross-device lifecycle. |
| F-043 | P1 | Mobile Family Hub creates groups with a fixed name and no visible idempotency/replay handling, conflates missing-group and other load failures, and exposes member-health/permission/chat actions without proven consent scope, ownership, revocation or audit controls. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/health/family-hub.tsx:72–115,177–195,221–300`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-hub.md` | Separate no-group from unavailable/error, define owner-scoped typed family contracts with idempotent creation, prove consent/role/revocation/audit and PHI minimization for member health sharing, and test guest/owner/member/stranger flows. |
| F-044 | P0 | Mobile Family Permissions falls back from any owner PATCH failure to an approval POST, reports success without proving semantic acceptance, and navigates away after failed member deletion; high-impact booking, pharmacy, payment, location and emergency scopes lack visible re-authentication, idempotency, versioning and audit controls. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/permissions.tsx:101–199,260–370`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-permissions.md` | Separate authorization from transport/contract errors, require explicit consent and step-up authentication for sensitive scopes, make grant/revoke/remove idempotent and auditable, preserve failure state, and test owner/member/stranger/unauth/replay/partial-failure paths. |
| F-045 | P1 | Mobile Family Member Health converts any member-records failure, including unauthorized/forbidden/not-found, into a normal empty health profile; proxy booking and family chat route without member identifier, consent context or proven active permission. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/member-health.tsx:29–50,78–117,276–290`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-member-health.md` | Preserve and distinguish 401/403/404/unavailable states, enforce server-scoped permissions and PHI minimization, and pass owner/member/consent context through proxy booking/chat with authorization and audit tests. |
| F-046 | P1 | Mobile Family Invite creates a bearer invite on mount before optional member metadata is submitted, exposes link/QR/code without proven expiry, one-time use, revoke, audience binding, rate limit, idempotency or audit controls, and shows copied state without a clipboard operation. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/invite.tsx:12–49,76–142`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-invite.md` | Bind invite metadata and audience server-side, define short-lived one-time/revocable invite lifecycle with abuse controls and audit, require idempotent creation, implement real clipboard behavior, and test owner/stranger/expired/replay/revoke flows. |
| F-047 | P1 | Mobile Family Permission Request calls `apiFetch` without a visible import under `@ts-nocheck`, falls back to the first pending request when `requestId` does not match, defaults all requested permissions to granted, and reports success after transport completion without semantic acceptance/error/idempotency/version/step-up/audit proof. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/permission-request.tsx:1–10,16–64`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-permission-request.md` | Resolve the API dependency/build gate, require an exact request ID, default to server-declared scopes with explicit consent, distinguish transport from domain acceptance, add error/replay/version/step-up/audit handling, and test owner/member/stranger/unauth/stale-request paths. |
| F-048 | P1 | Mobile Pharmacy Checkout calculates visible totals/fees from client cart and quote state, submits client item names/prices and discounts without a visible idempotency key, and navigates after order transport without proving payment authorization, insurance acceptance, order identity, or duplicate/replay protection across cash/card/wallet/split/insurance branches. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/pharmacy/checkout.tsx:47–89,120–177,301–333`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-pharmacy-checkout.md` | Make server quote/stock/price/payment state authoritative, require idempotent order creation and replay tests, verify non-empty order identity and branch-specific authorization before navigation, and expose truthful mismatch/partial/cancel/rejection states. |
| F-049 | P1 | Mobile Insurance conflates load/catalog failures with empty data, saves a client-derived policy payload with fixed `class: "A"` and no visible idempotency/re-auth/eligibility/document verification, then falls back to the request payload and reports success when the response is absent. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/profile/insurance.tsx:38–109,151–190`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-insurance.md` | Use typed server-owned policy status and error states, validate identifiers and documents, require idempotent re-authenticated save/update, prove eligibility/preauthorization/approval lifecycle, and never treat a local payload as persisted truth. |
| F-050 | P1 | Mobile Family Join performs membership mutation during code lookup, fabricates group/relation/empty permission data locally, leaves the visible accept step non-operative, and implements reject as navigation only; exact request identity, consent, expiry, replay and idempotency are not proven. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/join.tsx:29–70,175–225`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-join.md` | Separate preview from accept mutation, render server response, require explicit consent and exact invite/request IDs, implement reject/decline, expired/used/unauthorized/replay/idempotency states, and test membership transition/audit. |
| F-051 | P1 | Mobile Family QR Scan accepts arbitrary matching join URLs/raw codes without origin/signature/audience/expiry/one-time validation and only routes the code onward; invalid/permission/duplicate/error recovery is incomplete. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/scan.tsx:11–40,51–79`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-scan.md` | Validate signed Nabd payloads server-side, bind audience and lifecycle, enforce one-time/replay controls, distinguish invalid/expired/revoked/unauthorized states, and provide camera-denied/manual recovery paths. |
| F-052 | P1 | Mobile Family Chat uses five-second polling rather than proven realtime, sends text without visible idempotency/participant/moderation/attachment/read-delivery controls, swallows profile/member failures, and fabricates message IDs when the server omits them. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/family/chat.tsx:34–103,138–175`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-family-chat.md` | Bind chat to owner-scoped membership/consent, define realtime or explicitly bounded polling contract, require server IDs and idempotent send/replay tests, add moderation/PHI/error/delivery semantics, and distinguish membership revocation/forbidden states. |

| F-054 | P1 | Mobile Nursing Service Details converts provider API failure to an empty list, displays hard-coded licensing/coverage claims, uses unvalidated provider/price fields and local prescription gating, and routes to a profile without proven slot/quote/address/reservation/payment context. | `nabd_plus_patient_app/app/nursing/service-details.tsx:32–52,69–80,94–153,199–227`; `semantic-evidence-mobile-nursing-service-details.md` | Distinguish unavailable from empty, verify claims and provider fields, bind service/slot/quote/address and payment to server contracts, implement prescription upload/verification for required services, and test filters/empty/error/ownership/replay states. |

## Verification gaps (not findings yet)

These require source/contract/runtime evidence before classification: all six-locale translation completeness; accessibility labels and keyboard behavior; cache-control and PHI exposure in every helper; exact Backend ownership responses; migration/database evidence; outbox/event delivery; live payment provider settlement/refund; sandbox replay; Docker/CI; and visual parity against every Mobile screen.

## Product decisions required

The owner must decide launch scope for read-only versus commerce; pharmacy prescription acceptance and split-order rules; cash/insurance/payment/refund/expiry; home-care provider versus queue model; chat/realtime scope; family consent; notification update scope; indexing/noindex policy; and whether agent-native payment protocols (x402/MPP/UCP/ACP) are in the launch contract.

## Rule

A finding is not closed by a passing build or by a UI placeholder. Closure requires code evidence, contract evidence, security/ownership tests, state/error tests, locale/accessibility review, and live or approved sandbox evidence where the behavior is transactional.

## Finance Engine findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-099 | P0 | Finance Engine’s money-moving idempotency is implemented as check-then-write/saga sequences rather than an atomic reservation or transaction: ledger `exists` precedes append, refund prior-check precedes gateway/wallet/ledger work, provider debit checks precede append, loyalty refund checks precede credit/insert, and approval execution precedes status update. Replay or concurrency can duplicate money movement or leave divergent gateway, wallet, ledger, booking and operation state. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:101–104,404–423,549–615,617–637,829–850`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Introduce and prove atomic unique keys/conditional transitions or transactions plus an outbox/compensation strategy; test concurrent replay, crash after each side effect, gateway retry and exact-once financial outcomes. |
| F-100 | P1 | Financial database/aggregation failures are caught and converted into empty arrays or zero balances/reports, allowing unavailable data to appear as a valid zero/empty financial result. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:117–130,727–740`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Fail closed with explicit unavailable/error semantics, observability and retry; test database timeout/partial aggregate failure and ensure no zero-value success response. |
| F-101 | P1 | CancellationPolicy defaults unrecognized order states to an allowed full refund path, rather than rejecting unknown state; this is fail-open and can authorize cancellation for states not covered by the matrix. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:687–706`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Reject unknown states or require an explicit server-approved policy mapping; test every canonical, deprecated, malformed and unknown state with actor-role matrix. |
| F-102 | P1 | Provider-scoped coupons are rejected for a mismatch only when both coupon and request provider IDs are present; a missing request provider context can allow a provider-scoped coupon to validate. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:266–275`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Require provider context whenever the coupon is provider-scoped and derive it from authoritative cart/order lines; test missing, mismatched, cross-provider and mixed-cart cases. |
| F-103 | P1 | Admin financial mutations accept weakly typed arbitrary bodies/payloads, including approval type/payload and refund identifiers/amounts; resource/tenant binding, field allowlists, amount/currency constraints and request idempotency are not established at the controller boundary. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:941–970,973–1000`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Add typed DTOs and strict allowlists, server-derived actor/resource ownership, currency/amount validation, step-up approval and idempotency; test wrong-role/stranger/replay/conflict/amount-mismatch cases. |
| F-104 | P1 | Loyalty redemption/refund and fraud-alert/coupon usage de-duplication contain additional non-atomic sequences: points are debited before redemption transaction insertion, refund checks precede credit/insert, fraud alert find precedes insert, and coupon usage/counter rollback is not transactional. | `src/modules/finance-engine/finance-engine.module.ts:294–333,376–423,438–454`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-finance-engine-module.md` | Use unique constraints/conditional writes or transactions and compensate on failure; test concurrent redemption/refund, duplicate alert, coupon insertion failure and counter reconciliation. |


## Payments, Pharmacy Ops, and Wallet findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-105 | P0 | Paymob initiation trusts `payload.amount` directly, accepts `payload:any`, and creates an external order/payment key without booking/order ownership or server-authoritative amount resolution. A caller can request a charge for an arbitrary SAR amount and billing payload. | `src/modules/payments/paymob.service.ts:20–53`; `src/modules/payments/paymob.controller.ts:19–23`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-paymob-service.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-paymob-controller.md` | Resolve amount and owner from authoritative booking/order records, use strict DTOs, validate currency/amount/billing fields, and test owner/stranger/unauth/amount-tamper cases. |
| F-106 | P1 | Paymob and Payments payment-intent creation lack a complete exactly-once external-charge contract: Paymob has no visible idempotency or local intent reservation, while the generic Payments flow can leave an external intent and local transaction divergent after a post-gateway persistence failure. | `src/modules/payments/paymob.service.ts:20–53`; `src/modules/payments/payments.module.ts:160–217`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-paymob-service.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-payments-module.md` | Bind provider idempotency to a durable local intent, enforce same-key conflict semantics and unique active intent claims, and test concurrency, gateway success/local failure, retries and replays. |
| F-107 | P0 | Generic Payments refund accepts an optional amount without remaining-refundable-balance validation or refund idempotency and marks the booking `payment_status: refunded` even for a partial refund; gateway success followed by local failure can diverge settlement state. | `src/modules/payments/payments.module.ts:280–300`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-payments-module.md` | Enforce positive amount and remaining balance/currency, persist an atomic refund operation, distinguish partial/full booking states, and test replay, over-refund, concurrent refund and post-gateway failure. |
| F-108 | P1 | Verified Payments webhooks invoke payment verification and downstream booking/events/realtime/fraud side effects without event replay protection, timestamp/age validation or atomic state transition; duplicate valid callbacks can repeat effects. | `src/modules/payments/payments.module.ts:351–373,392–405`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-payments-module.md` | Store and uniquely claim provider event IDs, bind amount/currency/intent, apply monotonic state transitions transactionally, and test duplicate/out-of-order/aged callbacks. |
| F-109 | P1 | Pharmacy Ops substitute flow accepts client medicine identity, name, quantity and price, then recomputes order totals from that price; no authoritative catalog/quote or idempotent/versioned transition is established. | `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.service.ts:149–171`; `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.controller.ts:62–67`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-pharmacy-ops-service.md` | Resolve substitute from approved catalog and server quote, validate quantity/price/currency, require state/version/idempotency controls, and test price tampering and concurrent edits. |
| F-110 | P1 | Pharmacy Ops mutations for stock, item availability/quantity/substitution, basket submission/approval/rejection and insurance status use independent saves/events without visible CAS, idempotency or transactional compensation; concurrent or retried calls can produce divergent order/stock/event state. | `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.service.ts:83–105,118–147,174–232`; `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.controller.ts:50–75`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-pharmacy-ops-service.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-pharmacy-ops-controller.md` | Define state/version transitions and Idempotency-Key contracts, atomically couple order/stock/insurance changes with durable events, and test replay/concurrency/failure-between-writes. |
| F-111 | P1 | Provider pharmacy alias routes are guarded only by JwtAuthGuard in the controller while the canonical pharmacy controller has an explicit PHARMACY role; the alias exposes accept, basket, insurance and dispatch operations and may widen access if global role policy does not compensate. | `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.controller.ts:78–97`; `src/modules/pharmacy_ops/pharmacy_ops.module.ts:21–23`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-pharmacy-ops-controller.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-pharmacy-ops-module.md` | Enforce identical role/tenant/resource authorization on both surfaces and test unauth, wrong-role, stranger pharmacy, dispatch-driver and method/body parity. |
| F-112 | P1 | Legacy withdrawal schema permits only `pending|completed`, while the admin controller writes `rejected`; it has no amount/currency bounds, active-request uniqueness/idempotency, reservation/approval linkage or rejection actor/reason lifecycle. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/schemas/withdrawal-request.schema.ts:6–27`; `src/modules/admin-web-core/controllers/finance.controller.ts:71–147`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-withdrawal-request-schema.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-admin-finance-controller.md` | Align schema and controller state machine, add financial validation and unique idempotency/reservation references, enforce provider/bank verification and auditable rejection/approval transitions. |
| F-113 | P1 | Wallet and WalletTransaction schemas/repositories provide owner uniqueness for wallets but no currency/precision/amount bounds, duplicate reference/idempotency constraint, debit/CAS invariant, immutable ledger or walletId-to-owner binding; parallel repository paths can bypass assumed domain controls. | `src/schemas/wallet.schema.ts:7–55`; `src/modules/wallet/repositories/wallet.repository.ts:2–13`; `src/modules/wallet/repositories/wallettransaction.repository.ts:2–13`; `src/modules/nabd-extensions/repositories/wallet.repository.ts:2–13`; `src/modules/nabd-extensions/repositories/wallettransaction.repository.ts:2–13`; corresponding semantic evidence files | Enforce atomic balance/ledger transactions, unique reference claims, nonnegative/precise currency amounts, owner binding and immutable audit records across every repository path; test cross-path replay and negative-balance races. |
| F-114 | P2 | Wallet schema stores saved-card holderName and expiry alongside last4 without visible token/provider reference, encryption/redaction or PCI boundary; module wiring adds no card-data protection boundary. | `src/schemas/wallet.schema.ts:21–22`; `src/modules/wallet/wallet.module.ts:12–24`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-wallet-schema.md`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-wallet-module.md` | Store provider tokens only, minimize card metadata, define encryption/access/audit/retention controls and verify response projections and PCI scope. |


## Fraud alert model findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-115 | P1 | The backend defines two incompatible `FraudAlert` domain models under the same class name: admin-web-core uses entityId/entityName/type/flagReason/severity without an explicit collection, while the root schema uses userId/providerId/flagType/confidenceScore/status in explicit `fraud_alerts`; registrations can therefore diverge in field shape, collection behavior and lifecycle semantics. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/schemas/fraud-alert.schema.ts:6–24`; `src/schemas/fraud-alert.schema.ts:7–28`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-fraud-alert-duplicate-schemas.md` | Select one canonical model/collection and migrate all consumers; prove registration/schema parity, field compatibility and lifecycle behavior with integration tests. |
| F-116 | P1 | Root FraudAlert’s TypeScript union includes `payment_velocity_abuse` while the Mongoose enum allows only `duplicate_reviews_same_ip` and `rapid_bookings`, creating runtime rejection/type drift for a payment-fraud alert path. | `src/schemas/fraud-alert.schema.ts:18–19`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-fraud-alert-duplicate-schemas.md` | Align enum and type, define canonical alert taxonomy and test every supported/unsupported flag type. |
| F-117 | P1 | FraudAlert schemas have no event/source correlation, idempotency or deduplication key, actor/audit identity, expiry or evidence payload; repeated fraud detection can create non-reconcilable duplicate alerts across the parallel model paths. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/schemas/fraud-alert.schema.ts:6–24`; `src/schemas/fraud-alert.schema.ts:7–28`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-fraud-alert-duplicate-schemas.md` | Add unique source/event claims, actor/audit/expiry/evidence fields and an atomic deduplication policy shared across all consumers. |


## Procurement schema finding added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-118 | P1 | ProcurementRequest stores untyped `items`, an unrestricted zero-default warehouse quotation total and only `PENDING_ADMIN_REVIEW|QUOTATION_ISSUED|COMPLETED`; it lacks currency/precision/bounds, request idempotency/unique active claim, quote expiry/version, rejection/cancellation, approval actor and payment/settlement linkage. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/schemas/procurement-request.schema.ts:6–21`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b/backend/semantic-evidence-procurement-request-schema.md` | Define typed item/amount/currency contracts, canonical lifecycle and approval/rejection/expiry states, unique idempotent request claims, pharmacy/tenant binding and payment/settlement/audit linkage. |


## Medicines catalog findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-119 | P1 | `POST /medicines/admin/catalog` is declared twice with ADMIN role: one handler calls `adminCreateCatalog`, the later handler calls legacy `createCatalog`; registration order can make one unreachable or behavior-dependent while the two service paths apply different governance/default semantics. | `src/modules/medicines/medicines.controller.ts:246–250,305–308`; `src/modules/medicines/medicines.service.ts:843–860,1389–1418`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-medicines-controller.md` | Keep one canonical route/handler, remove ambiguity, document the accepted DTO/governance lifecycle and assert route uniqueness in controller/OpenAPI tests. |
| F-120 | P1 | Medicines mutations expose multiple raw inline object/`any` bodies (manual entry, admin edits/imports, suggestions, compare/barcode) without an explicit Idempotency-Key contract; service paths use spreads/coercion and separate writes, allowing unsafe fields, invalid zero prices, duplicate/replayed requests or partial state. | `src/modules/medicines/medicines.controller.ts:50–78,158–212,223–250,294–347`; `src/modules/medicines/medicines.service.ts:756–768,826–837,1117–1166,1300–1357`; corresponding semantic evidence files | Replace raw bodies with strict DTOs, enforce field/amount/image bounds and idempotency, add owner/role matrix and replay/concurrency/partial-failure tests. |
| F-121 | P1 | Shortage, image and catalog change workflows use read-then-insert/update sequences and fire-and-forget notifications/audit/storage events; concurrent reports/approvals or a failure between catalog/report/asset writes can duplicate alerts or leave live catalog, request state, storage and audit trail inconsistent. | `src/modules/medicines/medicines.service.ts:868–1000,1003–1105,1300–1357,1464–1513`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-medicines-service.md` | Atomically claim requests/state transitions, persist durable outbox/audit records, make storage operations compensatable, and test duplicate/concurrent/partial-failure/retry behavior. |
| F-122 | P2 | Public medicine barcode lookup falls back from exact governed barcode candidates to fuzzy name/ingredient regex matching, so malformed/textual scanner input can resolve to an unintended public medicine; public search also builds multiple regexes without a visible query-length budget. | `src/modules/medicines/medicines.service.ts:140–166,554–615`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-medicines-service.md` | Require strict barcode normalization/check digits and explicit fuzzy opt-in/threshold, bound query length/regex cost, and test ambiguous/malformed input and ReDoS-style cases. |
| F-123 | P2 | Public list/detail analytics derive user identity by decoding an unverified JWT payload; although not used as an authorization gate, forged claims can poison search/product-view attribution and associated recommendation/hot-medicine signals. | `src/modules/medicines/medicines.controller.ts:34–42`; `src/modules/medicines/medicines.service.ts:169–179,682–688`; `audit-artifacts/phase0b-backend/semantic-evidence-medicines-controller.md` | Use verified session identity or omit attribution for unauthenticated requests, and test forged/malformed tokens cannot affect user-linked analytics. |
