| F-583 | P0 | Coturn falls back to `turn.example.com` and `change_this_secret` when environment variables are absent, allowing invalid or predictable TURN credentials unless deployment configuration is perfect. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.service.ts:23–34` | Fail closed on missing/placeholder secret and validate production host/realm/ports. |
| F-584 | P0 | Credential TTL is caller-selectable inside the service with no visible upper/lower bound; a future caller can issue excessively long-lived TURN credentials. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.service.ts:47–65` | Enforce server-side bounded TTL tied to call window and test boundary values. |
| F-585 | P0 | ICE credentials are issued to any authenticated user without visible call/session membership, participant, purpose or account-state authorization. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.controller.ts:5–18; src/modules/coturn/coturn.service.ts:52–67` | Bind credential issuance to authorized call/session and test stranger/expired-call access. |
| F-586 | P1 | `/calls/ice/config` and `/calls/ice/credentials` expose TURN host/ports/realm and credential material through separate endpoints with no visible anti-leak, cache-control or one-time issuance policy. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.controller.ts:10–18` | Minimize endpoint surface, set no-store controls and test browser/log/proxy leakage. |
| F-587 | P1 | TURN URL overrides are accepted from a comma-separated environment string without visible scheme/host/port allowlist or secure transport enforcement. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.service.ts:30–44` | Validate ICE URLs and prohibit unsafe/unapproved endpoints in production. |
| F-588 | P1 | HMAC-SHA1 credentials are generated with raw authenticated user ID in username, with no visible pseudonymization, rotation key ID or credential issuance audit. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.service.ts:47–66` | Use controlled identity encoding/key rotation/audit and avoid unnecessary identifier disclosure. |
| F-589 | P1 | Both module and controller provide no visible dependency health/readiness or secret rotation lifecycle for TURN, while the module exports credential generation broadly. | `src/modules/coturn/coturn.module.ts:5–10` | Govern secret rotation, readiness and consumer scope; test rotation without call disruption. |

## Hospital administration findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-590 | P0 | `assertFacilityActor` returns when actor is absent, so any future/service caller that omits actor bypasses facility authorization; controller forwards raw user ID as hospital identity without explicit facility binding. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:33–40; src/modules/hospital/controllers/hospital.controller.ts:10–62` | Make actor/entity scope mandatory and test unauthenticated/non-member facility access. |
| F-591 | P0 | Branch, department and staff creation spread arbitrary request data into persistence, creating mass-assignment risk for hospital IDs, roles, permissions, status and unrelated schema fields. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:113–147` | Use typed allowlisted DTOs and immutable server-owned fields. |
| F-592 | P0 | Unified appointment listing accepts `branchId` directly without verifying that the branch belongs to the requested hospital, risking cross-facility appointment disclosure. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:162–170` | Validate branch-to-hospital relationship and test stranger/cross-facility access. |
| F-593 | P0 | Doctor onboarding sets profile affiliation and user `verified/active` directly without visible license, clinical review, institutional approval or audit workflow. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:149–159` | Require governed verification evidence, review state and immutable audit. |
| F-594 | P1 | Invitation creation/listing accepts raw role/identifier and returns entire invitation documents plus invitee/facility names; role values are not visibly allowlisted and PII minimization is absent. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:54–93; src/modules/hospital/controllers/hospital.controller.ts:64–79` | Typed role contract, minimum projections, invitation expiry/revocation and access tests. |
| F-595 | P1 | Invitation response changes status then separately updates user affiliation/permissions; replay/concurrency or partial failure can leave accepted invitation and missing/incorrect provider permissions. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:95–110` | Atomic pending transition plus membership grant, idempotency and reconciliation. |
| F-596 | P1 | Appointment status update accepts a fixed set but does not enforce a transition matrix, current-state predicate or event/audit, allowing backward/invalid operational state changes. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:172–186` | Use canonical workflow transitions, optimistic concurrency and audit/events. |
| F-597 | P0 | Wallet total sums completed appointment `total_price` in application memory, without ledger reconciliation, date/currency policy, refunds/chargebacks or bounded pagination. | `src/modules/hospital/services/hospital.service.ts:189–198` | Use canonical finance ledger, scoped period/currency and reconciliation tests. |
| F-598 | P1 | Hospital module exposes all service capabilities through one controller with no visible per-route role decorators; security depends on optional service actor semantics and caller-provided user context. | `src/modules/hospital/hospital.module.ts:14–30; src/modules/hospital/controllers/hospital.controller.ts:5–8` | Define route-level RBAC/entity scope and test every role/action matrix. |

## Custom services findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-599 | P0 | `GET /custom-services/admin/list` has no visible role guard and `adminList` accepts no actor, allowing any authenticated caller reaching the route to list cross-patient custom requests. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.controller.ts:16–18; src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:50–56` | Enforce admin/provider role and provider assignment/org scope; test unauth/stranger/non-provider access. |
| F-600 | P0 | Detail authorizes any provider-role actor without verifying assignment, facility, service kind or minimum purpose, risking cross-patient custom-request disclosure. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:43–48` | Implement provider-to-request scope and field-minimized projections. |
| F-601 | P0 | Creation persists patient name/phone, doctor notes/name, prescription image and arbitrary attachments from raw body without visible bounds, media validation, encryption/retention or consent policy. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:15–34` | Typed DTO, secure asset references/validation, PHI minimization and lifecycle controls. |
| F-602 | P1 | Custom-service creation has no visible idempotency/unique intent protection; retries/double taps can create duplicate patient requests and duplicate provider notifications. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:15–34` | Require idempotency key/unique intent and replay tests. |
| F-603 | P1 | Status update accepts any enum status without visible transition matrix, actor/assignment validation, current-state predicate or idempotency, so stale/concurrent actions can overwrite lifecycle. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:58–70` | Use canonical workflow transition, optimistic concurrency and audited replay tests. |
| F-604 | P1 | Status update returns persisted success and emits event without a durable outbox/retry contract; downstream notification/provider broadcast can be silently absent. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:32–34,64–70` | Persist event/outcome with outbox/retry and truthful response semantics. |
| F-605 | P1 | Raw priority and free-form names/notes/attachments have no visible allowlist/length/content validation beyond Arabic name presence, increasing abuse and unsafe-storage risk. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.service.ts:15–30` | Validate enums, lengths, content and attachment references. |
| F-606 | P1 | Module exposes one broad service/controller surface without visible explicit role policy for provider/admin operations, leaving authorization dependent on scattered service branches. | `src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.module.ts:8–15; src/modules/custom-services/custom-services.controller.ts:7–18` | Define route-level RBAC and security contract for every operation. |

## Events and catalog publication findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-607 | P0 | EventBus persists caller-supplied event type/entity/actor/before/after/meta without visible schema, size, field allowlist or PII redaction, making the global durable log a broad injection/disclosure surface. | `src/modules/events/event-bus.service.ts:7–21,36–51` | Typed event registry, payload bounds/redaction and consumer access policy. |
| F-608 | P1 | Event fanout failures are swallowed after persistence with no visible durable outbox/consumer retry/dead-letter state, so notifications/analytics may be permanently missed while the event appears complete. | `src/modules/events/event-bus.service.ts:62–69` | Durable delivery status/retry/reconciliation and truthful observability. |
| F-609 | P1 | Event idempotency is optional and duplicate handling relies on a Mongo duplicate error; event creation otherwise has no visible required command key or deterministic replay contract. | `src/modules/events/event-bus.service.ts:36–60` | Require idempotency for command events and test concurrent/replay behavior. |
| F-610 | P1 | Event listing permits up to 1000 broad records with filter values passed directly and excludes only `_id`/`__v`, risking PII exposure and heavy admin queries. | `src/modules/events/event-bus.service.ts:72–83` | Role/purpose projections, bounded pagination and sensitive-field policy. |
| F-611 | P0 | Catalog publication writes projection, invalidates caches and emits event as separate operations; partial failure can leave public discovery, SEO metadata and durable audit inconsistent. | `src/modules/events/catalog-publication.service.ts:77–143` | Use transactional/outbox/reconciliation workflow and failure tests. |
| F-612 | P1 | Publication actor ID/role/reason/idempotency are accepted from input without visible authorization or entity-level governance in the service, allowing a trusted caller to misattribute publication decisions. | `src/modules/events/catalog-publication.service.ts:9–17,122–143` | Enforce actor scope/review authority and immutable decision audit. |
| F-613 | P0 | Canonical path generation uses `slug || id` without visible typed uniqueness/collision validation, while source lookup uses ID; duplicate/cross-type slugs can produce incorrect canonical/index links. | `src/modules/events/catalog-publication.service.ts:51–60,77–87` | Enforce globally typed slugs/canonical collision checks and test all catalog types. |
| F-614 | P1 | Publication copies source provenance/review/indexing fields and derives robots/sitemap/feed inclusion without visible freshness, locale, deletion lifecycle or source-field completeness validation. | `src/modules/events/catalog-publication.service.ts:81–110` | Validate source governance, stale/deleted lifecycle and SEO contract before indexing. |

## Provider onboarding and contract findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-615 | P0 | Public onboarding start can create an active user/provider identity from phone/password/type without visible OTP, email verification, rate limit, abuse control or pre-registration policy. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:45–78,432–436` | Bind creation to verified identity and rate-limited onboarding contract. |
| F-616 | P0 | Onboarding step snapshots retain arbitrary submitted nested data, including potentially sensitive financial/identity/medical fields, with only narrow key/URI filters and no visible size/depth/retention controls. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:80–100,102–114,117–185` | Typed bounded snapshots, redaction policy, encryption/retention and access audit. |
| F-617 | P1 | `getMyProfile` and `getProgress` return broad provider profile documents rather than a minimum field contract, potentially exposing bank, license, identity and registration data to the provider surface. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:187–193,381–385` | Field-minimized DTO/projection and explicit sensitive-field policy. |
| F-618 | P0 | Submit marks onboarding pending and returns successfully even if moderation mirroring or contract generation fails because both operations are swallowed, leaving incomplete compliance artifacts. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:195–223` | Transactional/outbox workflow, degraded status and deterministic retry/reconciliation. |
| F-619 | P0 | Signature loading fetches arbitrary HTTP(S) URLs and storage external URLs without visible host allowlist, timeout, response-size/content-type restriction or SSRF protection. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/contract-pdf.service.ts:79–121` | Use internal object IDs/allowlist, bounded fetch and safe media validation. |
| F-620 | P1 | Contract PDF embeds provider email, phone, licenses and signer data and is stored/returned as raw base64, creating large response/log/cache and disclosure risk. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/contract-pdf.service.ts:155–166,211–217; provider-onboarding.module.ts:282–317,453–465` | Use secure object storage, signed short-lived download and minimum projections/no-store. |
| F-621 | P1 | Contract integrity hash is calculated from preliminary buffered bytes before `doc.end()` completes, then combines profile ID and truncates to 24 hex characters; the resulting digest is not a standard full-document hash. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/contract-pdf.service.ts:211–221` | Hash finalized PDF bytes with full-length algorithm and verify download integrity. |
| F-622 | P1 | Provider contract visibility defaults to `true` on insert, despite comments stating visibility should be an explicit admin grant, so a generated contract is provider-visible unless insert semantics are controlled exactly. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:249–269` | Default deny, explicit audited grant and owner-access tests. |
| F-623 | P0 | Moderation mirror marks email verified and carries password hash/phone into a second account collection without visible verification provenance, deduplication policy or cross-store transactional consistency. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:325–379` | Avoid credential duplication; bind verified claims and reconcile atomically/auditably. |
| F-624 | P1 | Resubmission can overwrite onboarding metadata while preserving approved/suspended status, creating inconsistent moderation state and stale contract/profile relationships. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:361–378` | Enforce explicit resubmission state machine and immutable review history. |
| F-625 | P1 | Unified search interpolates raw service query into regex across many provider fields and returns broad provider documents, enabling regex load abuse and unnecessary profile disclosure. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:391–428` | Escape/bound/index search, minimize projection and test adversarial queries. |
| F-626 | P1 | Onboarding and contract mutations have no visible idempotency key/current-state predicate; retries can duplicate users/profiles/events or regenerate/overwrite compliance artifacts. | `src/modules/provider-onboarding/provider-onboarding.module.ts:45–78,195–269,441–472` | Add command idempotency, unique constraints and replay/concurrency tests. |

## LiveKit webhook guard findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-627 | P0 | LiveKit webhook verification falls back to `fake_key`/`fake_secret` when environment secrets are absent, so a misconfigured deployment may start with predictable verification material. | `src/modules/webhooks/guards/livekit-webhook.guard.ts:8–13` | Fail closed at startup on missing/placeholder secrets and test configuration rejection. |
| F-628 | P0 | Signature verification assumes `request.body` is the exact raw signed payload, but the guard does not visibly enforce raw-body capture before JSON parsing or content/body-size limits. | `src/modules/webhooks/guards/livekit-webhook.guard.ts:15–27` | Enforce raw-body middleware, bounded payload, content type and canonical SDK verification tests. |
| F-629 | P0 | The guard accepts every cryptographically valid LiveKit event without visible room, participant, tenant or domain-entity binding; downstream handlers must not trust signature alone as authorization. | `src/modules/webhooks/guards/livekit-webhook.guard.ts:23–27` | Validate event issuer/room/participant against expected call/session and tenant. |
| F-630 | P1 | No visible timestamp tolerance, event ID/nonce deduplication or replay ledger is enforced, allowing a valid captured webhook to be replayed to downstream state handlers. | `src/modules/webhooks/guards/livekit-webhook.guard.ts:15–30` | Enforce freshness and durable idempotent event processing with replay tests. |
| F-631 | P1 | All receiver exceptions collapse to generic 401 and the verified event is attached as an unrestricted request property, with no visible observability classification or schema validation for downstream consumers. | `src/modules/webhooks/guards/livekit-webhook.guard.ts:23–30` | Validate event schema, classify failures safely and constrain downstream event contract. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-632 | P0 | Booking quote returns caller-supplied `price` as authoritative quote without visibly loading the booking/catalog price or creating a signed quote, enabling displayed price/payment-method divergence. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:176–201` | Derive price server-side from canonical booking/catalog and bind quote to entity/version. |

| F-634 | P0 | Insurance request duplicate protection is a read-before-create query without visible unique command key/current-state atomicity, allowing duplicate requests under concurrent retries. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:266–289` | Add unique intent/idempotency and concurrency tests. |
| F-635 | P1 | Insurance request stores patient name, policy object and arbitrary documents/history with no visible bounds, redaction, field-level access or retention policy. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:33–67,280–287` | Typed bounded documents, purpose projections, encryption/retention and access audit. |
| F-636 | P1 | Resubmit/appeal append caller-supplied documents and free-text notes, then save state and emit event separately; retries or event failure can diverge lifecycle and notifications. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:292–329` | Idempotent audited workflow/outbox and safe attachment contract. |
| F-637 | P1 | Provider decision and copay payment use read-then-save transitions without visible idempotency/current-state predicates; duplicate decisions/payments or concurrent operations can duplicate effects. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:365–421` | Atomic state transitions, command idempotency and replay/concurrency tests. |
| F-638 | P0 | Payment confirmation aliases (`insurance/payment-confirm`, `patient/pay-copay`) accept flexible request IDs and delegate to copay logic without a visible single canonical contract, increasing replay and routing ambiguity. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:495–517` | Consolidate endpoint/contract, require idempotency and verify payment binding. |
| F-639 | P1 | Copay settlement returns no visible durable failure state when payment event/request lookup or amount comparison fails; handler silently returns on mismatch and may require reconciliation. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:424–443` | Persist settlement outcome/dead-letter and expose reconciliation status. |
| F-640 | P1 | Patient cancellation changes insurance state without event emission, so provider/admin notifications and downstream booking state can remain stale. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:445–452` | Use canonical cancellation saga/outbox and truthful downstream status. |
| F-641 | P0 | Refund request trusts caller-supplied `amount_paid`, `scheduled_at` and payment ID; duplicate lookup is not visibly patient/merchant scoped, enabling amount/policy manipulation or cross-context collisions. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:544–563` | Load booking/payment truth server-side, scope identity and calculate policy from authoritative schedule. |
| F-642 | P0 | Admin finance/insurance controllers and finance accrual endpoint show JWT guards but no visible admin/provider role or ownership decorators; raw ledger accrual is exposed as an HTTP mutation. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:597–626` | Enforce route-level RBAC/service identity and isolate internal ledger commands. |
| F-643 | P0 | Refund decisions save state/history without visible role/ownership enforcement, idempotency or execution/ledger linkage, so any reachable guarded caller may decide refunds and leave financial state non-executed. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:570–581` | Separate approve/execute roles, atomic ledger linkage and audited state machine. |
| F-644 | P1 | Commission accrual uses read-before-create and caller-supplied amount/provider/service, with no visible currency/amount bounds or canonical payment binding; concurrent retries can race despite intended idempotency. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:129–149` | Bind to verified transaction, enforce unique key/amount/currency and reconcile ledger. |
| F-645 | P1 | Admin insurance stats and finance summaries aggregate broad request/ledger data without visible time/currency/tenant scope, making operational totals non-reproducible and potentially cross-tenant. | `src/modules/insurance-engine/insurance-engine.module.ts:342–356,160–167` | Define scoped periods/currencies, ledger truth and auditable report snapshots. |

## Admin governance findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-646 | P0 | Patient 360 returns broad user/profile-adjacent data, five domain histories and recent events without visible purpose-based field minimization or tenant scope. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:102–159` | Enforce least-privilege projections, case justification, tenant scope and access audit. |
| F-647 | P0 | Entity trace accepts arbitrary entity type/ID and returns entity, all matching events and state history without visible caller/entity authorization, enabling cross-patient/provider trace disclosure. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:161–183,233–242` | Require scoped trace permissions and minimum redacted output with owner/stranger tests. |
| F-648 | P1 | Provider-performance and commission reports perform per-provider domain queries and compute heuristic/application-memory metrics, creating N+1 load and non-reproducible totals. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:65–100,301–335` | Use bounded aggregations/materialized reports with snapshot/version/reconciliation. |
| F-649 | P1 | Patient spend estimate sums booking/order prices without refunds, discounts, currency, insurance/copay or payment truth, yet is exposed as a patient financial metric. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:130–142` | Label/derive from canonical transaction ledger with currency and refund adjustments. |
| F-650 | P1 | Global summary uses estimated counts and heterogeneous state alias sets, so active/total counts may be stale or semantically inconsistent across domains. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:185–227` | Define versioned universal state mapping and auditable count semantics. |
| F-651 | P0 | Kill-switch listing/toggle has only JWT guard and no visible admin role, key allowlist failure, reason requirement, idempotency, audit or current-state predicate for high-impact controls including payments/emergency. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:244–286` | Enforce privileged RBAC, two-person/approval policy where required, audit and atomic toggles. |
| F-652 | P0 | Kill-switch toggle silently succeeds for an unknown key and returns current configuration, hiding operator error and making control-plane actions non-truthful. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:270–285` | Reject unknown keys with explicit error and record action outcome. |
| F-653 | P1 | Kill-switch list lazily creates persistent defaults during a GET, coupling read access to database mutation and risking race-created duplicate/default state. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:261–267` | Versioned migration/initialization with unique key and read-only GET semantics. |
| F-654 | P0 | Commission report uses hard-coded type default rates and profile rates without visible currency/ledger/refund/tax policy, while earnings are rounded in application code. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:301–335` | Use canonical commission rules/ledger with versioned rates and reconciliation. |
| F-655 | P0 | Commission update accepts raw number without visible non-negative/max/range validation, effective date, approval, idempotency or audit, enabling arbitrary financial policy mutation. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:338–343` | Typed bounded change contract with approval/effective period/audit. |
| F-656 | P1 | Commission list queries all provider profiles and then each domain sequentially with a fixed 30-day window and no pagination/currency/tenant parameter, causing scale and report-boundary issues. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:301–335` | Bounded paginated/reporting query with explicit scope and period. |
| F-657 | P1 | Admin governance module globally wires broad models/controllers including B2B and system config, increasing privilege coupling and making route-level policy difficult to verify from the module boundary. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:345–365` | Separate control planes or define explicit per-capability authorization contracts. |
| F-658 | P1 | Universal normalization and score calculations rely on event/state-history presence and heuristic thresholds; missing history is treated as zero/healthy rather than unknown, risking misleading operator decisions. | `src/modules/admin-governance/admin-governance.module.ts:38–63,115–120` | Distinguish unknown/incomplete data, disclose confidence and test missing-history behavior. |

## Facility operations findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-659 | P0 | Facility operations controllers use JWT only and derive facility scope from mutable account fields without visible role/membership/entity verification, so authenticated users may reach facility actions outside their organization. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:253–339,345–418` | Enforce facility membership/role and test cross-facility owner/stranger access. |
| F-660 | P0 | Admission updates bed, creates admission and decrements ward availability as separate writes; concurrent admissions or partial failure can double-occupy beds and corrupt counters. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:82–109` | Use atomic reservation/transaction/current-state predicate and reconciliation. |
| F-661 | P0 | Discharge updates admission, bed and ward count separately, creating partial-failure/counter drift and replay races even though already-discharged is checked before writes. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:112–150` | Use atomic discharge workflow, idempotency and counter repair. |
| F-662 | P1 | Ward creation inserts a ward then creates one bed per loop without total-beds bounds or rollback, allowing oversized requests and partially created wards. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:60–79` | Bound capacity and use transactional/batch creation with reconciliation. |
| F-663 | P1 | Admission listing returns patient IDs, names/phone fallback and discharge summaries without visible purpose-based minimization or clinical retention policy. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:29–50` | Minimum clinical projection, access audit and retention controls. |
| F-664 | P1 | Shift creation spreads raw body data into a shift record with no visible allowlist, time/day/department/user relationship validation or idempotency. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:161–171,289–302` | Typed bounded DTO, relationship validation and replay protection. |
| F-665 | P1 | Attendance check-in accepts caller-provided latitude/longitude without bounds, geofence, timestamp or duplicate-shift validation and creates unlimited attendance rows. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:182–191,309–312` | Validate location/time, enforce policy/geofence and unique attendance intent. |
| F-666 | P1 | Attendance checkout checks only facility and record ID, with no current-state predicate, actor/shift ownership or idempotency, allowing unauthorized/repeated checkout. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:194–204,314–317` | Enforce worker/shift ownership and atomic one-time checkout. |
| F-667 | P0 | Surgery booking uses read-before-create room conflict detection without atomic lock; concurrent requests can double-book an operating room. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:213–241` | Atomic reservation/unique interval strategy and concurrency tests. |
| F-668 | P0 | Surgery booking accepts patient/surgeon/assistants, room, date and duration without visible facility relationship, authorization, date/duration bounds or patient consent validation. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:207–241,325–333` | Typed clinical scheduling contract, linked-entity authorization and audit. |
| F-669 | P1 | Facility announcements/resources use raw collections and JWT-only access with no visible role separation, idempotency, moderation, branch ownership or resource type allowlist. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:342–418` | Govern communications/resources by role, branch and lifecycle contract. |
| F-670 | P1 | Resource creation coerces invalid capacity to 1 and silently accepts arbitrary type strings; update silently ignores invalid status while returning only a generic result contract. | `src/modules/facility-ops/facility-ops.module.ts:383–417` | Validate/reject invalid values explicitly and return truthful mutation outcomes. |

## Slot locks findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-671 | P0 | Reserve performs overlap query then create separately; concurrent patients can both pass collision check and create overlapping held locks. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:20–31` | Use atomic reservation/unique strategy and concurrency tests. |
| F-672 | P0 | Reserve accepts invalid dates, end-before-start, unbounded duration and arbitrary booking kind/provider ID with no visible provider-slot relationship validation. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:14–18` | Validate interval, type, provider/slot contract and strict bounds. |
| F-673 | P0 | Confirm attaches caller-supplied booking ID to a lock without verifying that booking belongs to the patient/provider/kind/interval or is still payable/bookable. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:34–42` | Bind lock confirmation to canonical booking transaction and authorization. |
| F-674 | P1 | Confirmed locks live for one year with no visible completion/cancellation release lifecycle, allowing stale locks to block availability long after a service ends. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:38–41` | Release on terminal booking state with bounded fallback expiry and reconciliation. |
| F-675 | P1 | Expired lock cleanup is a broad deleteMany executed during reserve, while TTL index behavior is assumed; cleanup failure is not surfaced and may add latency/race effects. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:18–20` | Rely on governed TTL/index and monitor/reconcile cleanup separately. |
| F-676 | P1 | Release returns `{ok:true}` when lock is missing and uses read-then-save without idempotency/current-state predicate, making repeated or stale releases non-auditable. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:45–51` | Define truthful idempotent release contract and atomic state update. |
| F-677 | P1 | Mine returns all active locks with no visible pagination, projection, ordering or expiration filtering beyond status, exposing raw reservation data and permitting unbounded growth. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:54` | Use bounded minimum projection and server-side expiry/index semantics. |
| F-678 | P1 | Slot-lock mutations use raw bodies and no visible `Idempotency-Key`, so retries/double taps can create or confirm unintended reservation state. | `src/modules/slot-locks/slot-locks.module.ts:57–65` | Require command idempotency and replay/timeout recovery tests. |

## Admin roles guard findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-679 | P0 | Routes without handler `@Roles` metadata are allowed by default, and the guard does not visibly require an authenticated user for non-`/admin` paths. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:23–41` | Separate authentication from authorization and fail closed for protected routes. |
| F-680 | P0 | Admin protection depends on a string heuristic over `request.route.path || request.url`; rewrites, prefixes, adapter differences or missing route metadata can bypass the `/admin` fallback. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:34–39` | Use route metadata/policy registry, not URL-string detection, with adapter tests. |
| F-681 | P1 | `Roles` writes metadata only to the handler and `RolesGuard` reads only handler metadata; class/controller-level role declarations are not visibly inherited or merged. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:12–16,29–32` | Merge class/handler metadata deterministically and test inheritance/override semantics. |
| F-682 | P1 | Local `UserRole` enum may drift from common role/effective-role vocabulary, creating inconsistent authorization decisions across modules. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:3–10,26–31` | Use one canonical role registry and compatibility tests. |
| F-683 | P1 | Effective-role expansion is trusted without visible tenant/account-state/expiry validation; inherited roles may grant admin/provider privileges beyond current membership. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:26–31` | Validate role provenance, tenant scope, status and expiry before authorization. |
| F-684 | P1 | The guard itself has no visible global registration evidence or route policy inventory, so controller decorator drift can silently create unprotected high-impact endpoints. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/guards/roles.guard.ts:19–41` | Register globally/explicitly, generate route-policy inventory and enforce coverage gates. |

## API security findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-685 | P0 | Blacklist and rate-limit protection fail open when Redis is unavailable: `isBlacklisted` returns false and `checkRate` allows the request. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:72–100` | Define fail-closed/degraded policy by route class and test dependency loss. |
| F-686 | P0 | Per-IP/user/device counters are incremented sequentially with separate expiry operations and no visible atomic script, so concurrent requests can bypass limits or leave inconsistent windows. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:101–123` | Use atomic Redis operation with bounded failure handling and deterministic tests. |
| F-687 | P1 | `x-device-id` is trusted as a caller-provided identity key for rate limits and device blacklisting, allowing rotation/spoofing and collateral blocks. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:72–90,101–117` | Bind device identity to attested/session-bound identifier and avoid security decisions on raw header alone. |
| F-688 | P1 | Honeypot hits are intentionally returned as fake 200 success with empty data, making observability/client truthfulness ambiguous and potentially masking proxy/scanner mistakes. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:154–164` | Use explicit isolated sink/telemetry policy without contaminating product semantics. |
| F-689 | P1 | Route-class limits and enumeration detection rely on raw URL regexes/numeric path segments, vulnerable to route variants, encoded forms and false positives; fixed limits lack tenant/service context. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:24–32,86–145` | Use canonical route identity and versioned policy with adversarial/variant tests. |
| F-690 | P1 | Security events persist IP, device ID, user-agent, path and arbitrary extra fields without visible minimization, retention, access classification or redaction. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:54–70` | Define security-log schema, retention/encryption and least-privilege access. |
| F-691 | P0 | Global middleware executes for every route, including potentially health, webhook, auth and internal paths, without visible exclusions or trust-boundary policy; dependency/ordering effects may break critical integrations. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:217–226` | Explicit route matrix and integration tests for auth/webhook/health/CORS behavior. |
| F-692 | P0 | Rate-limit middleware short-circuits before Nest CORS and manually reflects a configurable/default origin list, creating policy drift and possible credentialed-origin exposure. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:166–184` | Centralize CORS policy and test disallowed/allowed credentialed responses. |
| F-693 | P1 | Blacklisting and rate-abuse logging are best-effort around Redis/event failures, with no durable reconciliation or operator-visible status when the security action cannot be persisted. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:80–84,111–119,154–168` | Durable abuse decision/event path or explicit degraded-security alerting. |
| F-694 | P0 | Blacklist clear accepts a raw key and deletes both IP and device namespaces, with no visible key-type validation, reason, audit event, approval or current-state result. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:209–213` | Privileged audited clear contract with explicit namespace and outcome. |
| F-695 | P1 | Admin security events returns raw event rows and grouped counts without time filters, pagination for counts, tenant scope or sensitive-field projection beyond Mongo `_id`. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:197–207` | Bounded redacted reporting with period/scope and auditable access. |
| F-696 | P1 | Global middleware trusts `req.ip` and connection fallback without visible proxy trust configuration, so deployment topology can alter rate-limit/blacklist identity. | `src/modules/api-security/api-security.module.ts:58–65,87–94,156–161` | Explicit proxy/IP canonicalization and deployment-specific tests. |

## Consistency layer findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-697 | P1 | Duplicate detection is heuristic by patient/provider/kind/day and excludes only literal `CANCELLED`, so legitimate multiple bookings may be flagged and domain-specific overlaps missed. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:34–59` | Use domain-specific uniqueness/overlap rules and classify confidence. |
| F-698 | P1 | Orphan, missing-event and mismatch audits inspect bounded samples (500/200) and then truncate results, producing false negatives while returning no completeness indicator. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:61–109` | Paginate/checkpoint full scans and report coverage/cursor. |
| F-699 | P0 | Orphan fixer can cancel bookings across five domains with separate updates, no current-state/payment/refund/notification checks and no transaction, causing irreversible financial/state side effects. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:139–157` | Require explicit approval, canonical domain cancellation saga, dry-run evidence and atomic reconciliation. |
| F-700 | P0 | Reconciliation emits missing birth events one by one with no visible unique event constraint/idempotency or durable failure queue; reruns may duplicate lifecycle events or silently lose failures. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:126–137` | Add event uniqueness/outbox and durable retry/reconciliation report. |
| F-701 | P1 | Mismatch detection treats a cancelled state without a `service.cancelled` event as the sole inconsistency and does not reconcile other state/history/payment divergences. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:77–92` | Versioned invariant matrix covering all lifecycle/payment transitions. |
| F-702 | P1 | Stuck detection covers only orders and home-care with broad regexes and creation time, omitting other domains, scheduled time, SLA context and terminal repair path. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:111–120` | Define per-domain SLA/state checks and actionable remediation. |
| F-703 | P1 | Reconcile/fix results swallow per-record failures or return mixed action results without a durable job identity, retry cursor or operator approval record. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:126–157` | Durable job/audit contract with failure taxonomy and resume semantics. |
| F-704 | P0 | `fix-orphans` becomes destructive solely when query `dry_run=false`; no second confirmation, change ticket, maximum batch or idempotency is visible at the controller boundary. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:160–168` | Require explicit privileged command contract, bounded batch and approval/idempotency. |
| F-705 | P1 | Consistency service relies on raw model fields/state aliases and system events without tenant/account scope, so cross-domain or cross-tenant records may be mixed in reports and repairs. | `src/modules/consistency/consistency.module.ts:39–120,139–157` | Add tenant scope and canonical projections/invariants. |

## Service catalog findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-706 | P0 | Provider catalog creation spreads the raw body into Lab/Radiology service documents, allowing mass assignment of fields outside the intended contract. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:77–90` | Use typed allowlisted DTO/projection and reject unknown fields. |
| F-707 | P0 | Catalog creation, ownership creation and event emission are separate/best-effort operations; partial failure can leave orphan service records or owner mappings. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:81–90` | Use transaction/outbox/reconciliation and explicit failure state. |
| F-708 | P0 | Service update accepts arbitrary patch fields and deletion removes catalog/ownership separately, enabling sensitive field mutation and consistency drift. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:93–116` | Typed patch contract, immutable fields, atomic delete/tombstone and audit. |
| F-709 | P1 | Provider role allowlist includes broad roles without visible provider-account/entity relationship checks beyond ownership mapping, so role alone may grant catalog operations. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:63–75` | Verify active account, entity type and ownership/tenant membership. |
| F-710 | P1 | Admin catalog listing returns up to 500 broad documents and ownership records while accepting raw regex search, creating disclosure, regex-load and pagination risks. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:119–127` | Escape/bound indexed search, minimum projection and paginated scope. |
| F-711 | P0 | Admin approval toggles service `active` based on a boolean body with no visible state/version predicate, review reason, idempotency or approval audit. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:130–136,199–202` | Versioned approval command with RBAC, reason, audit and replay protection. |
| F-712 | P1 | Schedule GET mutates persistence by creating a default schedule, and schedule upsert accepts weekly/blocked/numeric structures without visible interval, timezone, date or bounds validation. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:138–155` | Read-only GET, typed schedule schema, timezone/interval validation and migration. |
| F-713 | P1 | Availability uses JavaScript local-day/time helpers and caller date without explicit provider timezone or invalid-date handling, so DST/date boundary behavior can produce incorrect slots. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:49–51,158–181` | Canonical timezone/date contract with DST and boundary tests. |
| F-714 | P0 | Available-slot counts are checked sequentially against a booking counter and are not reservations; concurrent callers can both receive/consume the same capacity. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:167–181` | Couple availability with atomic slot reservation/lock and capacity tests. |
| F-715 | P1 | Schedule and catalog mutations have no visible `Idempotency-Key`; retries can duplicate schedules/services/events or replay state changes. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:77–116,149–155,190–202` | Require command idempotency and replay tests. |
| F-716 | P1 | `:type` and `:entity` values are accepted as raw strings at controller boundary, with service behavior selecting models/defaults rather than rejecting all unsupported values. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:185–202` | Validate enum path parameters and return explicit 400 for unsupported values. |
| F-717 | P1 | Catalog approval/ownership events are emitted asynchronously with ignored failures, so public visibility/search and moderation state may diverge from the intended audit trail. | `src/modules/service-catalog/service-catalog.module.ts:88–90,99–100,114–115,132–134,153–154` | Durable event/outbox delivery and publication reconciliation. |

## Home-care compatibility findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-718 | P0 | Home-care booking trusts caller address, payment method and scheduled date while deriving only service price from the server; no visible canonical quote, timezone, address ownership or payment-policy binding exists. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:74–95` | Bind booking to validated address/quote/payment contract and slot lock. |
| F-719 | P0 | Booking creation uses a plain create with no visible idempotency or slot-capacity check, so retries/double taps can create duplicate visits. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:74–95` | Require Idempotency-Key and atomic capacity reservation/replay tests. |
| F-720 | P1 | Provider and service detail routes return broad documents, while catalog reads have no visible pagination or minimum field projection beyond Mongo metadata. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:32–57` | Public/provider DTO projections, pagination and PII policy. |
| F-721 | P0 | Unassigned nursing requests are intentionally visible to every eligible nursing provider, and transition/assignment logic lacks an atomic claim predicate; concurrent nurses can claim the same visit. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:103–113,116–156` | Atomic claim/lease with provider eligibility and concurrency tests. |
| F-722 | P0 | Nursing state transitions use read-then-save and accept raw metadata/fields without idempotency/version checks, allowing invalid concurrent transitions or duplicated side effects. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:116–148` | Versioned state machine, atomic transition and command idempotency. |
| F-723 | P0 | GPS, check-in and visit-report paths store coordinates, vitals, clinical notes, medications and procedure details with no visible schema bounds, consent, geofence, encryption or retention policy. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:158–180` | Typed clinical/GPS contracts with consent, bounds, access audit and retention. |
| F-724 | P1 | GPS update checks numeric type but not latitude/longitude ranges, timestamp freshness, accuracy or rate, enabling invalid location and storage abuse. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:162–167` | Validate ranges/accuracy/freshness and rate-limit tracking. |
| F-725 | P1 | Care-plan creation assignment check is role-dependent and doctors/hospitals can create plans without visible booking/patient relationship verification; tasks are caller-supplied clinical instructions. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:182–206` | Verify clinical relationship/consent and audit author role; typed plan schema. |
| F-726 | P1 | Availability update uses `updateOne` and always returns success without matched-count verification, so a provider profile mismatch can be reported as a successful mutation. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:209–212` | Return truthful matched result and enforce profile ownership. |
| F-727 | P0 | Inventory requests append arbitrary raw items to a booking without quantity/type/stock validation, atomic reservation, duplicate prevention or idempotency. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:215–224` | Typed bounded inventory command, stock truth, atomic append and replay protection. |
| F-728 | P1 | Nursing checklist and supplies endpoints expose hard-coded reference data, with no version/provenance/locale or clinical protocol governance. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:228–272` | Versioned governed reference data and locale-specific contracts. |
| F-729 | P1 | Chat aliases expose multiple conventions and accept several body fields, increasing contract drift; alias mutations have no visible idempotency or unified error semantics. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:274–308` | One canonical chat command contract with compatibility adapters and replay tests. |
| F-730 | P0 | Provider quick-send and message aliases trust thread IDs/body content at this boundary; security depends entirely on downstream ChatService and is not locally evident, making ownership regressions hard to detect. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:289–307` | Explicit thread membership/owner tests at canonical service boundary. |
| F-731 | P1 | Event emission for booking/state changes is optional/best-effort and swallowed, so notifications, dispatch and audit state may diverge from persisted booking state. | `src/modules/home-care-compat/home-care-compat.module.ts:94–95,131–138` | Durable outbox/event delivery and reconciliation status. |

## Admin web core module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-732 | P1 | Admin Web Core co-wires seven high-impact controllers and eleven operational/financial schemas without module-boundary policy, making protection/projection drift dependent on each controller. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:24–49` | Generate route-policy inventory and enforce per-controller RBAC/projection gates. |
| F-733 | P0 | FinanceEngineModule and provider-ops withdrawal schema are imported together with commission/withdrawal/admin schemas, creating duplicate financial ownership surfaces that can diverge in writes and truth. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:11–22,27–39` | Establish one canonical ledger/withdrawal owner and cross-module invariant tests. |
| F-734 | P1 | The module exposes broad sensitive models (audit, fraud, procurement, provider, emergency, appointment) to a shared admin context without visible field-level separation or least-privilege repository boundary. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:11–39` | Split bounded contexts and minimum projections by controller/capability. |
| F-735 | P1 | No module-local providers/exports, audit interceptor, transaction policy or explicit RolesGuard registration is visible, so critical guarantees cannot be verified from the module boundary. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:24–51` | Make security/transaction wiring explicit and verifiable in route inventory. |
| F-736 | P1 | Controller wiring includes multiple control planes (system health/config, moderation, governance and extended operations) under one module, increasing blast radius of deployment/configuration mistakes. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:41–49` | Isolate control planes or enforce capability-scoped modules and approvals. |
| F-737 | P1 | Duplicate schema/model registration across imported modules is not resolved at this boundary, risking divergent collection assumptions, repository injection order and inconsistent financial/operational behavior. | `src/modules/admin-web-core/admin-web-core.module.ts:22–39` | Inventory model registration globally and enforce single-owner schema contracts. |

| F-741 | P1 | Top-searched terms are returned from raw search analytics without visible sensitive-term policy, normalization guarantees, tenant scope, retention or privacy thresholding. | `src/modules/analytics/analytics.module.ts:18–25` | Apply privacy/redaction thresholds, retention and scoped analytics contract. |

| F-745 | P1 | Analytics overview returns financial/operational metrics without period, currency, tenant, refund, insurance/copay or payment settlement scope and without metric confidence/provenance. | `src/modules/analytics/analytics.module.ts:70–116` | Versioned report contract with scope, source, freshness and reconciliation. |
