| F-747 | P1 | Module has no visible audit interceptor, report snapshot or tenant policy; sensitive search/chat/user-derived data relies only on controller ADMIN metadata. | `src/modules/analytics/analytics.module.ts:120–149` | Add least-privilege projection, report access audit and tenant scoping. |
| F-748 | P1 | Collection and field names are hard-coded across analytics methods, so schema/status drift can silently yield zeros or misleading metrics without health/version detection. | `src/modules/analytics/analytics.module.ts:16–116` | Add schema contracts, metric health checks and explicit unknown-state handling. |

## Unified booking flow findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-749 | P0 | Booking-flow provider detection and ownership use broad role/provider-type and OR-field heuristics without visible active-account, tenant or canonical provider relationship validation. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:42–64` | Use canonical capability/tenant membership checks and owner/stranger tests. |
| F-750 | P1 | Status steps are derived from event presence while universal/domain state is read separately; missing/late events can make the patient-facing progress contract contradict actual state. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:103–169` | Define state as canonical source and expose event freshness/incomplete status. |
| F-751 | P1 | Status exposes provider name, phone, city and rating plus domain totals/schedule without visible role-based projection or sensitivity policy. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:130–169` | Minimum projection by actor and purpose with PII classification. |
| F-752 | P1 | Timeline returns complete state history and all events for an entity with no visible pagination, redaction, retention or event-type allowlist. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:172–180` | Bounded redacted timeline contract and access audit. |
| F-753 | P0 | Retry invokes a same-state workflow operation and swallows engine errors before returning `{ok:true}`, so it can claim dispatch when no retry/broadcast occurred. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:183–200` | Return durable command/job status and never report success after swallowed failure. |
| F-754 | P1 | Retry has no visible idempotency key, retry budget, cooldown or concurrency predicate, allowing repeated user requests to fan out duplicate signals. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:183–200,232–240` | Idempotent bounded recovery command with replay/cooldown tests. |
| F-755 | P0 | Admin resolve accepts force-complete/cancel and arbitrary reason, then updates the domain model separately without checking matched count or binding payment/refund/notification side effects. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:202–229` | Privileged audited resolution saga with atomic domain/payment/notification outcomes. |
| F-756 | P0 | `resolve` relies on manual role check in service while controller has only JWT guard; route policy can drift and non-admin callers receive inconsistent error semantics. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:202–208,232–239` | Enforce centralized RBAC metadata and negative authorization tests. |
| F-757 | P1 | Admin force resolution can write a target status without validating legal domain transition or terminal/payment constraints, risking false completion/cancellation. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:209–228` | Apply versioned per-domain transition and settlement rules. |
| F-758 | P1 | Status total is selected from raw `total/totals.total/price` fields without canonical transaction/refund/insurance/currency semantics. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:154–169` | Bind financial display to verified quote/ledger and currency contract. |
| F-759 | P1 | Entity and event retrieval use raw type/ID aliases and return broad event/state data, with no visible identifier format validation or tenant scope. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:55–73,161–180` | Validate IDs/types and enforce tenant/entity scope. |
| F-760 | P1 | Module centralizes five domain models and workflow engine but has no visible idempotency, transaction, projection or event-delivery policy at its boundary, increasing orchestration drift. | `src/modules/booking-flow/booking-flow.module.ts:242–259` | Define explicit orchestration contracts and cross-domain invariant gates. |

## Workflow engine findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-761 | P0 | `apply` validates universal states but does not atomically persist entity state and event; caller mutation and event emission can diverge. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:226–277` | Transaction/outbox or durable command state with invariant tests. |
| F-762 | P0 | Event emission errors are swallowed for successful transitions, birth events and rollback events, allowing state changes without durable lifecycle evidence or alerting. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:239–274,303–320` | Durable outbox/retry and truthful command/event status. |
| F-763 | P1 | Same-universal-state transitions return valid silently, allowing materially different domain transitions to bypass domain-specific legality and audit semantics. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:178–197` | Define explicit no-op vs domain-transition policy and tests. |
| F-764 | P1 | Universal state mappings compress many domain states into one state and unknown values throw, so clients may lose actionable distinctions and mapping drift can become runtime failure. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:37–162` | Versioned mapping contract with completeness/compatibility tests. |
| F-765 | P0 | Workflow engine does not enforce current-state/version predicate or idempotency around supplied `mutate`, so concurrent callers can overwrite transitions or replay them. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:226–258,284–297` | Atomic compare-and-set, command idempotency and replay tests. |
| F-766 | P1 | Rollback event records raw error message in event metadata, risking internal/database/provider disclosure to downstream consumers and logs. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:259–274` | Error taxonomy/redaction and secure event projection. |
| F-767 | P1 | Provider matching returns broad provider profile documents with only license documents excluded; remaining PII and internal fields may be disclosed to matching callers. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:354–357,460–471` | Explicit patient-safe provider projection and field-level policy. |
| F-768 | P0 | Insurance company/network/class matching dereferences contract fields and arrays without visible shape validation, and `insurance_company` scoring can award 20 points after filter logic without independent verified acceptance semantics. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:366–391,432–440` | Typed insurance contract, verified coverage and adversarial input tests. |
| F-769 | P1 | Provider ranking accepts location coordinates without range validation and gives unknown distance a positive default score, potentially ranking non-local/invalid-location providers above truthful results. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:449–458` | Validate coordinates and define unknown-distance policy as explicit/neutral. |
| F-770 | P1 | Ranking uses active status and heuristic fields such as online/working-hours as availability without calendar/capacity/slot truth, so score does not establish bookability. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:442–447` | Couple score to verified slot/capacity service and freshness. |
| F-771 | P1 | Provider/facility matching can perform sequential per-candidate facility queries after a broad 120-profile scan, creating N+1 latency and load risk. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:354–357,393–409,474–478` | Batch/aggregate bounded matching with workload budget. |
| F-772 | P0 | Orchestration emits requested/matched events before ranking completes, swallows event failures and returns a trace that is not visibly persisted or idempotent. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:481–515` | Persist orchestration job/trace, emit durable events and expose truthful state. |
| F-773 | P1 | Orchestration trace includes a patient ID prefix and timestamp, creating a correlatable identifier and no visible collision/idempotency guarantee. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:487–498` | Use opaque cryptographic trace ID and idempotent request key. |
| F-774 | P1 | Public lifecycle and universal endpoints expose complete internal state maps/transitions and accept raw kind/state, enabling contract discovery and inconsistent errors for unsupported values. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:530–559` | Publish versioned public contract projection and validate enums. |
| F-775 | P1 | Match endpoint accepts raw body, has no visible `CurrentUser`/patient binding, idempotency, budget or output projection at controller boundary. | `src/modules/workflow-engine/workflow-engine.module.ts:560–578` | Typed bounded match command with caller scope, rate/cost controls and safe response. |

## Cart and pharmacy checkout findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-776 | P0 | Generic cart line creation trusts caller-supplied name, price, kind, payment method, notes and arbitrary metadata; a client can create misleading or underpriced cart lines. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:77–102` | Resolve every payable line from canonical catalog/quote and use typed allowlisted DTOs. |
| F-777 | P0 | Cart summary recomputes totals from stored line prices/quantities and hard-codes home-visit fee 50 without canonical quote, tax, currency, insurance or fee source. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:62–75` | Use server quote/ledger source with currency/fee version and expiry. |
| F-778 | P0 | `ensureCart` and whole-document saves use read-then-create/read-then-save without visible unique-race or version handling, allowing duplicate carts and lost concurrent line updates. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:51–54,77–101,127–153` | Atomic upsert/versioned line commands and concurrency tests. |
| F-779 | P1 | Legacy line add/update/remove/clear routes lack visible idempotency while contract routes have it, leaving duplicate-tap/retry behavior inconsistent across clients. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:206–226` | Require idempotency on all mutations or explicitly deprecate legacy routes. |
| F-780 | P1 | Generic line update accepts payment/insurance/home-visit/notes without visible enum, quantity, provider, insurance or service compatibility validation. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:127–137,220–222` | Typed field validation and server policy resolution. |
| F-781 | P0 | Pharmacy checkout creates an order and clears the cart in separate operations; failures between them can produce duplicate orders on retry or a paid/order state with uncleared cart. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:190–202` | Idempotent checkout saga/transaction with durable outcome and retry semantics. |
| F-782 | P0 | Checkout is cash-only and rejects prescription media, so the contract does not complete supported payment/insurance/prescription journeys and must not be presented as full production commerce. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:167–174,195–202` | Publish capability state and implement verified payment/insurance/prescription contracts before activation. |
| F-783 | P0 | Checkout does not visibly refresh/revalidate line prices, medicine availability, prescription requirement or stock immediately before order creation. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:176–197` | Canonical server-side quote, stock/reservation and prescription validation at checkout. |
| F-784 | P1 | Address validation checks profile membership and numeric lat/lng but does not visibly validate coordinate ranges, address freshness, delivery eligibility or canonical address snapshot/version. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:183–195` | Typed address/delivery-zone validation and immutable order snapshot. |
| F-785 | P1 | Manual pharmacy lines use generated service IDs and zero price with pending review, but checkout forwards them to OrdersService without visible review/approval gate or customer-facing pending semantics. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:120–124,190–197` | Block/route manual items through explicit review workflow before payable order creation. |
| F-786 | P1 | Contract update route maps only `quantity` while legacy update accepts broader fields, creating divergent cart contracts and potentially bypassing payment/insurance policy through legacy paths. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:216–222` | Unify mutation schema/policy and deprecate incompatible aliases. |
| F-787 | P1 | Prescription projection selects the newest active prescription without pagination, validity/ownership detail, clinician status or medication reconciliation and exposes manual-entry flags without contract version. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:227–245` | Versioned patient-safe prescription/read contract with validity and reconciliation rules. |
| F-788 | P1 | Checkout accepts a coupon code and forwards it without visible validation, ownership, expiry, currency or discount truth in the returned total. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:167–197,202` | Resolve coupon server-side and return verified quote/discount breakdown. |
| F-789 | P1 | Cart schema stores arbitrary `meta` and line notes and summary returns the whole cart object, risking sensitive/internal metadata disclosure to the patient client. | `src/modules/cart/cart.module.ts:23–36,62–75` | Explicit response projection and metadata classification/redaction. |

## Patient UX/refund/override findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-790 | P1 | Review upsert accepts raw comment/aspects and identifies uniqueness only by booking kind/ID without visible provider aggregation, idempotency or immutable audit semantics. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:36–67` | Typed review contract, content policy, unique key and audited moderation. |
| F-791 | P0 | Refund request accepts caller-supplied amount and stores it without visible cap, quote/ledger comparison, currency or payment-provider authorization. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:69–79` | Derive refundable amount from canonical settlement and enforce cap/currency. |
| F-792 | P0 | Refund request creation and existing-request check are not atomic/uniquely constrained; concurrent retries can create duplicate requests or divergent amounts. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:75–79` | Unique compound constraint and idempotent command. |
| F-793 | P0 | Approved refund updates request state and booking payment fields in separate best-effort operations without provider/ledger settlement or compensation. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:93–123` | Atomic/durable refund saga with provider and ledger truth. |
| F-794 | P1 | Admin refund list returns up to 500 raw refund records with no visible projection, pagination, tenant scope, retention or sensitive-field policy. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:86–91` | Least-privilege paginated refund queue with scope/audit. |
| F-795 | P0 | Rebook deep-clones prior booking fields and writes a new record with raw requested date and generic states, without slot/capacity, current price, payment, insurance or prescription revalidation. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:126–140` | Rebook through canonical booking/quote/slot contracts and idempotency. |
| F-796 | P1 | Rebook removes only selected fields and may carry forward stale address, provider, clinical, insurance or arbitrary internal data from the original document. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:131–138` | Explicit allowlisted clone projection and data-classification tests. |
| F-797 | P0 | Admin force-cancel directly mutates state/payment-adjacent fields without transition legality, current-state predicate, refund/notification orchestration or idempotency. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:191–216,271–280` | Versioned audited cancellation command with settlement semantics. |
| F-798 | P0 | Admin force-transition accepts arbitrary target state and writes both `state` and lowercase `status`, allowing invalid completion/delivery/payment state combinations. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:218–241,281–285` | Per-domain transition allowlist and invariant validation. |
| F-799 | P0 | Admin mark-payment can set paid/refunded/failed and optional amount directly on any entity without payment gateway verification, ledger entry, currency/cap or idempotency. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:243–267,286–290` | Payment truth only from verified provider/ledger or controlled compensating workflow. |
| F-800 | P1 | Manual override routes require a reason but have no visible dual control, approval ticket, bounded batch or immutable local audit record; event logging failures are swallowed. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:204–215,229–240,255–267` | Segregation of duties, immutable audit and durable event delivery. |
| F-801 | P1 | Admin override model lookup is by raw entity ID across several domains without visible tenant/provider scope, increasing cross-domain ID collision and wrong-entity mutation risk. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:172–189,191–267` | Validate domain/type and scoped canonical identifiers. |
| F-802 | P1 | Shared `refundrequests` collection uses a distinct Mongoose model name because schema-name collision previously stripped fields silently, indicating unresolved cross-module schema ownership/drift risk. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:293–310` | One canonical refund schema/owner with migration and compatibility tests. |
| F-803 | P1 | Patient UX mutations have no visible idempotency interceptor at controller boundary, including review/refund/rebook and admin refund/override operations. | `src/modules/patient-ux/patient-ux.module.ts:144–164,271–290` | Require idempotency and replay tests for all commands, including admin actions. |

## Operations safety findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-804 | P1 | SLA report samples at most 500 records per domain and returns patient IDs without completeness/cursor, tenant scope, privacy projection or invalid-state handling. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:56–81` | Paginated complete report with scoped/redacted output and explicit invalid-state metrics. |
| F-805 | P1 | SLA timing uses `updatedAt`/`createdAt` and fixed universal limits without scheduled-time, timezone, pause, holiday or domain policy version semantics. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:34–70` | Versioned per-domain SLA clock with timezone/pause rules. |
| F-806 | P0 | Escalation returns `re-broadcast-requested` results but does not visibly enqueue, mutate or invoke a rebroadcast, creating false operational success. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:83–100` | Durable escalation command/job with truthful execution status. |
| F-807 | P1 | Escalation accepts an unbounded caller threshold and heuristic raw state regex, allowing overly broad/late/early operational actions. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:83–98` | Typed bounded threshold and canonical state/SLA policy. |
| F-808 | P0 | Penalty amount is derived from caller-supplied scheduled/cancelled timestamps and fixed constants without policy version, currency, waiver/grace or payment/ledger settlement. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:102–114` | Server-owned booking timestamps/policy and canonical penalty settlement. |
| F-809 | P0 | Penalty creation has no visible unique key/idempotency/current-state predicate; retries or repeated domain callbacks can create duplicate charges. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:102–114` | Unique booking/kind penalty constraint and replay tests. |
| F-810 | P1 | Fallback ranking returns candidate providers but does not reserve capacity, assign the booking, verify availability freshness or notify actors; result is not equivalent to fallback execution. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:116–125` | Atomic provider lease/reassignment workflow and truthful state. |
| F-811 | P1 | Fallback accepts raw kind/city/insurance/service keys and relies on ranking projection without visible caller/booking relationship, tenant or patient consent scope. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:116–125` | Bind fallback to scoped booking and typed criteria. |
| F-812 | P1 | Provider fallback output can include broad provider objects from workflow ranking, risking internal/PII disclosure to callers or admin clients. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:116–125` | Safe provider projection by actor/purpose. |
| F-813 | P1 | Penalty list accepts raw status/patient filters and returns up to 100 records with no visible tenant scope, pagination cursor, projection or audit. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:128–146` | Scoped paginated penalty queue with least-privilege fields and access audit. |
| F-814 | P0 | All operations-safety mutations lack visible idempotency, including escalation, penalty assessment and fallback, so retries can duplicate side effects or produce contradictory operator output. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:137–146` | Require command idempotency and replay tests. |
| F-815 | P1 | Module depends on workflow universal mapping but has no visible reconciliation/handling for unknown or newly introduced domain states; one bad record can reject report collection. | `src/modules/operations-safety/operations-safety.module.ts:65–80` | Isolate invalid records, alert and preserve report completeness. |

## Admin authority findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-816 | P0 | Admin force cancel/complete/reassign operations mutate domain state directly without legal transition, current-state/version predicate, idempotency or payment/refund/notification orchestration. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:50–83` | Use canonical versioned transition/saga contract with replay protection. |
| F-817 | P0 | Lab/radiology insurance overrides directly change insurance status and rejection reason without insurer verification, copay/settlement recomputation, approval separation or idempotency. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:106–145` | Require verified payer workflow, dual approval and ledger reconciliation. |
| F-818 | P0 | Appointment force reschedule accepts raw new time and sets `RESCHEDULED` without slot lock, capacity, timezone, current-state or payment policy validation. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:173–184` | Reuse canonical reschedule contract with lock/capacity and audit. |
| F-819 | P0 | Admin force confirm/complete/cancel can write terminal or confirmed states with no per-domain legal transition and no completion evidence, risking false operational truth. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:50–70,85–104,117–136,150–171` | Enforce per-domain state/required-evidence invariants. |
| F-820 | P1 | Admin reassign accepts raw pharmacy ID and sets `PHARMACY_RECEIVED` without verifying active/eligible pharmacy, capacity, acceptance or patient notification. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:73–83` | Verify target provider and perform atomic reassignment workflow. |
| F-821 | P0 | `logAction` swallows both database and event failures, so high-impact manual actions can execute without durable audit evidence or operator alert. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:45–48` | Make audit append durable/mandatory with failure quarantine. |
| F-822 | P1 | Audit log stores admin name, before/after snapshots, reason and target data as arbitrary objects without visible redaction, retention, immutability or tenant policy. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:18–30,45–48` | Typed redacted immutable audit schema with retention and access control. |
| F-823 | P0 | Impersonation signs a JWT containing target email/phone/role/permissions and admin identity and returns it in the response, with no visible short TTL, purpose/audience, revocation, target restrictions or cookie-only delivery. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:206–239` | Brokered support session with short TTL, explicit impersonation claims, revocation and no reusable token exposure. |
| F-824 | P0 | Impersonation permits any target user ID after target existence check; no visible consent, approval, reason validation, support scope or protection for privileged/admin targets. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:207–227` | Restrict target classes and require approved support case/dual control. |
| F-825 | P1 | Provider suspension toggles only the User active flag without profile/license/booking/financial reconciliation, potentially leaving suspended providers bookable or paid. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:187–203` | Canonical provider lifecycle transition and downstream reconciliation. |
| F-826 | P1 | Unsuspend requires no reason or visible review/credential revalidation and emits the same best-effort audit path. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:197–203` | Require approval/revalidation and immutable reasoned audit. |
| F-827 | P1 | Action listing accepts raw filters and returns up to 500 broad snapshots with no visible pagination cursor, time/tenant scope or field-level projection. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:241–247,275` | Scoped paginated redacted audit query. |
| F-828 | P0 | All authority mutations are POST endpoints with raw bodies and no visible `Idempotency-Key`, allowing duplicate manual state/payment/insurance actions on retries. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:251–273` | Require command idempotency and replay tests for every action. |
| F-829 | P1 | Controllers pass empty default reasons (`b.reason || ''`) for many destructive operations, weakening rationale quality and audit accountability. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:256–272` | Require non-empty structured reason/ticket for all high-impact commands. |
| F-830 | P1 | Raw target IDs are looked up across multiple domain models without visible tenant/account scope, increasing wrong-entity/cross-tenant mutation risk. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:51–147,151–184,188–203` | Validate domain-scoped opaque IDs and tenant/provider boundaries. |
| F-831 | P1 | Module centralizes many sensitive domain models and manual control operations but exposes no transaction/event-outbox/approval policy at the module boundary. | `src/modules/admin-authority/admin-authority.module.ts:278–291` | Make control-plane invariants explicit and verifiable. |

## Event reliability findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-832 | P0 | Event delivery records use payload ID or timestamp/random fallback with no visible unique event/listener key, allowing duplicate confirmations and unreliable delivery counts. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:49–60` | Unique event/listener/attempt identity and deterministic delivery semantics. |
| F-833 | P0 | DLQ persists complete event payload and raw error text without visible redaction, encryption, retention or tenant/access policy, risking clinical/financial/PII exposure. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:17–27,62–74` | Classified redacted payload storage with retention and least privilege. |
| F-834 | P1 | Delivery listener swallows persistence failures, so a successfully observed event may be reported as reliable without a delivery-log record or alert. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:49–60` | Durable failure signal and reconciliation queue. |
| F-835 | P1 | Reliability status returns global pending/dead DLQ entries and recent raw records without tenant/time scope, pagination cursor or sensitive projection. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:76–97` | Scoped redacted operational report with bounded pagination. |
| F-836 | P1 | Status `failed` delivery count is not visibly populated by this listener, while event total uses estimated document count; metrics may be internally inconsistent. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:76–96` | Define metric sources and reconciliation/health indicators. |
| F-837 | P0 | DLQ retry loads a shared pending batch without claim/lease/version predicate; concurrent workers can emit the same event multiple times and overwrite attempts/status. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:99–118` | Atomic claim/lease and compare-and-set status transitions. |
| F-838 | P1 | A single successful retry marks an entry `retried` without durable downstream listener acknowledgments or per-listener outcome, so partial delivery can be misreported as recovered. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:103–117` | Track listener-level acknowledgments and completion criteria. |
| F-839 | P1 | Retry marks entries dead after five failures without visible exponential backoff, retry classification, operator approval or recovery policy for transient outages. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:110–117` | Versioned retry/backoff/dead-letter policy with manual recovery. |
| F-840 | P0 | Replay emits a stored event directly with no replay marker, deduplication, side-effect safety check, idempotency or event-age/authorization policy; replay can repeat financial/clinical mutations. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:120–128` | Replay envelope, idempotent consumers, approval and side-effect classification. |
| F-841 | P1 | Replay returns a raw error string on failure, potentially exposing internal details through an admin endpoint and event operational logs. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:124–128` | Redacted error taxonomy and correlation ID. |
| F-842 | P0 | Retry/replay mutations have no visible `Idempotency-Key`, approval/dual control or bounded operation contract at the controller boundary. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:131–139` | Require audited idempotent command workflow. |
| F-843 | P1 | Event reliability module has no visible retention/archival/index policy for delivery/DLQ collections, creating unbounded sensitive-event storage and operational load. | `src/modules/event-reliability/event-reliability.module.ts:17–38,76–97` | Define TTL/archival, indexes, deletion governance and capacity alerts. |

## Security module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-844 | P0 | Audit writes swallow persistence failures for every event, so security/financial/medical actions can occur without durable audit evidence or alerting. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:11–16,55–99` | Durable mandatory audit path with failure quarantine/alert. |
| F-845 | P1 | Audit entries accept arbitrary details and capture amounts, provider account IDs, patient IDs, phone-derived data and medical-report metadata without visible redaction, classification, encryption or retention. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:14–16,85–99` | Typed event projections with least data, retention and access policy. |
| F-846 | P1 | Admin audit search builds raw regexes from caller input over action/resource fields, creating regex-load/semantic abuse risk and no visible indexed search contract. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:19–39` | Escaped bounded search with indexed fields and query budgets. |
| F-847 | P1 | Admin/recent/critical queries return raw audit documents with no visible field projection, tenant scope, time cursor or immutable-access audit. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:17–53,161–167` | Scoped redacted paginated audit views with access logging. |
| F-848 | P1 | Personal activity endpoint exposes all records for `user_id` without event sensitivity filtering, redaction or a documented patient-facing audit contract. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:42–53,152–159` | Patient-safe event allowlist and projection. |
| F-849 | P1 | Event handlers trust heterogeneous payload shapes and IDs; missing/mismatched fields can create misleading audit records without schema validation or provenance. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:55–99` | Versioned event schemas and rejected/unknown-event metrics. |
| F-850 | P1 | `x-correlation-id` is accepted and reflected without length/character/format validation, allowing log injection, cardinality abuse or trace spoofing. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:101–108` | Generate/bind validated opaque correlation IDs and preserve trusted parent trace separately. |
| F-851 | P1 | Slow/error logging writes raw method and URL to console, potentially leaking query PII/tokens and bypassing structured redaction/retention controls. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:109–115` | Structured redacted logging with secret/query suppression. |
| F-852 | P1 | Security headers omit CSP, Permissions-Policy and explicit cache controls; HSTS is hard-coded globally without visible proxy/deployment policy. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:120–132` | Complete deployment-aware header policy with browser/security tests. |
| F-853 | P1 | Global tracing/header middleware applies to every route without visible exclusions/order policy, risking webhook/auth/health/static compatibility and inconsistent security behavior. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:170–180` | Explicit middleware route matrix and integration tests. |
| F-854 | P0 | Method-level `@Roles` protection depends on guard/metadata integration not visible at this module boundary; recent/critical/admin routes lack an explicit class-level admin guard contract. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:134–168` | Verify global RolesGuard and negative tests for every admin endpoint. |
| F-855 | P1 | Audit query pagination uses `skip` and count over unbounded collection, enabling high-offset load and inconsistent snapshots under concurrent writes. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:19–53` | Cursor pagination, bounded query budgets and snapshot/freshness semantics. |
| F-856 | P0 | Sensitive events such as payment completion/refund, insurance decisions and medical reports are audited without visible tenant/patient consent/access policy, making secondary data exposure likely. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:63,82,87–97` | Purpose/actor-based access control and minimum event payloads. |
| F-857 | P1 | Audit service has no visible unique event key/deduplication or correlation persistence, so repeated event delivery can create duplicate records and hinder incident reconstruction. | `src/modules/security/security.module.ts:12–17,55–99` | Idempotent event audit key and durable correlation metadata. |

## Admin notification center findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-858 | P0 | Broad campaign audiences (`all`, patients/providers/roles) resolve up to 100,000 raw user IDs without visible notification consent, suppression, jurisdiction/tenant or preference checks. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:93–119` | Enforce consent/preferences/suppression and scoped audience policy. |
| F-859 | P1 | Campaign deep-link params are arbitrary objects and only route string shape is checked; unsafe/internal parameters can be broadcast to clients. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:38,67–90,194–198` | Typed allowlisted deep-link schemas and route capability policy. |
| F-860 | P1 | Campaign IDs use timestamp/random strings and creation has no visible unique-retry/idempotency or duplicate-name/business-key control. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:136–155` | Opaque unique ID plus idempotent create command. |
| F-861 | P0 | Campaign send uses read-then-update to `sending` without claim/version predicate; concurrent callers can send the same campaign repeatedly. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:184–191` | Atomic claim/lease and idempotent send workflow. |
| F-862 | P0 | Campaign send marks `sent` with `sent=queued` and `failed=0` after sequential queue calls; it does not establish device/provider delivery, and partial failures can leave misleading status/stats. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:192–212` | Distinguish queued/accepted/delivered/failed with durable per-recipient outcomes. |
| F-863 | P1 | Campaign cancellation has no visible reason, audit event, idempotency or race-safe operator approval beyond a status predicate. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:215–220,401–407` | Audited versioned cancel command with replay protection. |
| F-864 | P0 | Scheduled campaign job reads up to 10 due rows without claim/lease and invokes send separately, allowing duplicate execution across workers/ticks. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:231–242` | Atomic scheduler claim and job identity. |
| F-865 | P0 | Appointment reminders queue and then set `reminder_24h_sent` in separate operations without claim/idempotency, so concurrent workers or queue failure can duplicate or falsely suppress reminders. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:244–280` | Atomic reminder claim, delivery outcome and retry policy. |
| F-866 | P1 | Reminder timing uses server Date window and no visible appointment/provider/patient timezone, consent, quiet-hours, cancellation or delivery preference checks. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:246–278` | Canonical timezone/notification-preference policy and tests. |
| F-867 | P0 | Appointment reminder resolves doctor profiles sequentially per appointment, creating N+1 load and possible identity mismatch while sending a clinical-context notification. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:259–275` | Batch projection and verified appointment/provider binding. |
| F-868 | P0 | Retargeting queries legacy `carts` and unpaid-order status heuristics, sends marketing-like messages and marks rows after queueing without claim/idempotency, consent or payment truth. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:283–329` | Canonical cart/order/payment states, opt-in policy, claim and delivery semantics. |
| F-869 | P1 | Retargeting notification text includes an emoji and fixed Arabic copy, with no locale/template governance despite a multi-language platform requirement. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:299–304,319–324` | Versioned localized templates and professional channel-specific content. |
| F-870 | P1 | Campaign and overall analytics aggregate raw push logs/engagements without campaign/tenant scope, deduplicated recipients, consent cohort or freshness/source metadata. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:174–182,332–364` | Canonical recipient/event denominator and scoped auditable metrics. |
| F-871 | P0 | Broadcast/campaign/send/retarget mutations have no visible `Idempotency-Key`, dual approval or durable admin action audit at controller boundary. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:367–412` | Require idempotent audited commands and approval for broad audiences. |
| F-872 | P1 | Campaign listing/detail and segment counts expose operational audience/campaign data without visible tenant/access scope, deletion/retention policy or field-level projection beyond `_id`. | `src/modules/admin-notification-center/admin-notification-center.module.ts:121–132,158–181,367–399` | Least-privilege scoped projections, retention and audit access. |

## Provider operations findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-873 | P1 | Provider leave creation/cancellation lacks idempotency and cancellation returns success without verifying a matched record, allowing duplicate or misleading availability changes. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:40–81,528–532` | Unique command key, matched-count checks and availability reconciliation. |
| F-874 | P1 | Prescription templates and saved diagnoses accept raw nested item/body objects with limited validation; diagnosis search interpolates raw regex and may enable query abuse. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:83–111` | Typed schemas, escaped bounded search and clinical content policy. |
| F-875 | P0 | Lab QC locates bookings by raw ID without visible provider/facility role or ownership scope, allowing unauthorized clinical state/verification changes if route access is obtained. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:173–218,545–546` | Scope booking to authorized lab/provider and enforce transition policy. |
| F-876 | P0 | Lab QC updates booking, sends critical notifications and credits earnings in separate operations; partial failure can create clinical/financial inconsistency and notification loss. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:198–224` | Durable clinical event + idempotent settlement saga. |
| F-877 | P1 | Critical-value notification embeds raw notes and booking context, while notification persistence swallows errors and has no visible localization/consent/suppression policy. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:201–235` | Redacted localized critical-alert workflow with durable delivery. |
| F-878 | P0 | Nursing checklist updates any home-care booking by ID without visible nurse assignment, patient ownership, state or phase-order checks. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:237–245,548–552` | Assignment-scoped state machine and immutable checklist history. |
| F-879 | P0 | Nursing signature stores up to 300,000 base64 characters and marks service completed before separate earnings credit, without signature media validation, patient/visit binding or transaction. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:247–266` | Verified signature artifact, visit scope and atomic completion/settlement. |
| F-880 | P0 | Nursing tracking accepts arbitrary numeric lat/lng without range, timestamp, geofence, rate or nurse/booking ownership validation. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:268–275` | Validated bounded GPS stream with assignment/geofence policy. |
| F-881 | P0 | Nursing escalation updates any booking by ID and writes critical admin notification separately without state/role scope or durable alert guarantee. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:277–290` | Scoped emergency transition with durable on-call escalation. |
| F-882 | P1 | Ambulance ETA trusts caller coordinates and a fixed 40 km/h estimate, without coordinate validation, route provider/source, freshness or uncertainty disclosure. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:292–305,554–555` | Validated source coordinates and truthful ETA provenance/uncertainty. |
| F-883 | P0 | Ambulance handover/completion update emergency records by raw ID without visible driver assignment/state checks; completion report accepts arbitrary vitals and body data. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:307–327` | Assignment-scoped emergency state machine and typed clinical handover schema. |
| F-884 | P0 | Ambulance earnings use fare/amount/body amount fallback and separate ledger insertion, creating client-controlled financial truth and duplicate/race risk. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:316–327,483–503` | Canonical fare settlement, unique ledger constraint and transaction/saga. |
| F-885 | P1 | Invoice PDF reveals patient ID and raw order/state/financial fields; invoice total/commission/VAT are assembled from document values rather than a verified invoice/ZATCA source. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:329–347` | Canonical invoice service with least data and tax/ledger verification. |
| F-886 | P1 | Invoice authorization permits patient, admin or pharmacy based on raw ID comparison but lacks explicit provider/facility tenancy, order visibility and audit policy. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:330–334,559–565` | Scoped invoice authorization and access audit. |
| F-887 | P1 | Provider settings and compatibility schedule endpoints persist arbitrary values/bodies without typed schema, allowed-key policy or optimistic concurrency. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:432–444,613–627` | Versioned typed settings with allowlisted keys and validation. |
| F-888 | P1 | `statsToday` scans multiple legacy collections/ID fields, swallows missing-collection errors and derives revenue from a limited wallet page, so displayed statistics may be incomplete or misleading. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:381–400` | Canonical provider metrics with source/coverage metadata. |
| F-889 | P1 | Provider reviews return broad raw rating records and reply mutation has no visible idempotency/content policy beyond non-empty text. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:402–429,604–611` | Redacted review projection and idempotent moderated reply command. |
| F-890 | P0 | Consultation end accepts raw notes/diagnosis/prescription, marks appointment complete, writes clinical documents and credits earnings in separate operations; body amount can influence financial credit. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:446–504,629–632` | Typed clinical contract, authorization/state checks and atomic completion/ledger workflow. |
| F-891 | P0 | Most provider-ops mutation routes lack visible `Idempotency-Key` and route-level provider/assignment guards, including QC, nursing, ambulance, settings and consultation completion. | `src/modules/provider-ops/provider-ops.module.ts:521–632` | Require idempotency, provider role/assignment and owner/stranger negative tests. |

## Doctors module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-892 | P0 | Source contains realistic demo doctors, fees, ratings, clinics and biographies; an environment flag can seed them, creating a production false-data hazard if enabled or misconfigured. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:10–17,42–49` | Remove production seed path or hard fail outside isolated test environment with provenance checks. |
| F-893 | P1 | Public doctor list uses raw regex from search/insurance input and returns up to 100 records without escaped bounded search, cursor pagination or canonical public projection. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:56–65,246–254` | Typed escaped search, pagination and safe public DTO. |
| F-894 | P1 | Specialty listing performs per-specialty sample/count queries and includes records without the same published/accepting/deleted filter, causing N+1 and catalog inconsistency. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:74–82` | Canonical filtered aggregation with bounded query plan. |
| F-895 | P1 | Slot generation depends on local JavaScript date/time helpers and raw date input without explicit timezone/date validation, risking wrong-day and DST behavior. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:27–29,85–110` | Provider/patient timezone contract and deterministic slot tests. |
| F-896 | P0 | Slot availability is calculated by read/count before appointment insert without atomic slot lock or unique capacity constraint, allowing overbooking under concurrency. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:105–124` | Atomic capacity claim/lock and concurrent booking tests. |
| F-897 | P0 | Booking accepts raw type/contact/documents/reason/address/payment/insurance data and derives fee from profile without canonical quote, payment/insurance verification or typed clinical/media validation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:112–140` | Typed booking/quote contract with server-owned price and payment policy. |
| F-898 | P0 | Appointment creation and provider/patient notifications are separate; event/notification failures are swallowed and no idempotency key is visible. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:124–145,52–54` | Durable idempotent booking saga with truthful notification status. |
| F-899 | P1 | Doctor inbox exposes all appointments to admin and provider scope is based on doctor profiles by account, with no visible facility/tenant/patient data projection. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:152–158` | Scoped role/tenant projection and access audit. |
| F-900 | P0 | Chat listing checks patient ownership only for patient role; message posting verifies existence but not participant, assigned doctor or appointment state, allowing unauthorized conversation writes by authenticated users. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:182–196,262–264` | Participant/assignment/state authorization and owner/stranger tests. |
| F-901 | P0 | Consultation note upsert allows any provider/doctor/admin role for any appointment and merges arbitrary body fields into a clinical note without assignment, schema or immutable-version checks. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:198–212,264` | Assigned clinician authorization, typed clinical DTO and append/version semantics. |
| F-902 | P1 | Chat content filter is a narrow regex for phone/URLs/messaging brands, not a complete moderation/privacy policy; messages are stored raw. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:190–196` | Classified content policy, redaction/retention and abuse controls. |
| F-903 | P1 | Appointment detail returns raw appointment, doctor and consultation note documents with no explicit field-level patient/provider projection. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:173–180,258–260` | Actor-specific DTOs and medical-data minimization. |
| F-904 | P1 | Patient appointment list and chat/notification lists use fixed limits without cursor/total/freshness semantics, causing silent truncation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:148–150,183–187,214–221` | Cursor pagination and explicit completeness metadata. |
| F-905 | P1 | Notification mark-read returns `{ok:true}` regardless of matched record, making nonexistent/foreign IDs indistinguishable from successful mutation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:224–231` | Return matched outcome and enforce idempotent read command. |
| F-906 | P1 | Notification writes swallow persistence failures and store raw title/body/deep-link data without visible localization, redaction or delivery semantics. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:52–54,214–232` | Durable localized notification contract and failure handling. |
| F-907 | P1 | Availability updates all doctor profiles for an account and emits an event without visible version/idempotency or schedule/booking reconciliation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:234–243,266` | Scoped versioned availability command with downstream reconciliation. |
| F-908 | P1 | Controllers use raw bodies/queries and expose many mutations without visible `Idempotency-Key`, DTO validation or route-level role/assignment guards. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.module.ts:246–277` | Typed contracts, idempotency and explicit route authorization matrix. |

## Business rules findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-909 | P0 | Surge configuration is mutable in process memory through a JWT-guarded but not visibly admin-restricted route, with no persistence, versioning, bounds, audit or idempotency. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:41–48,167–178` | Admin-scoped versioned persisted policy with bounds and audit. |
| F-910 | P0 | Rule validation accepts caller-controlled service price, patient demographics, insurance context, provider capabilities and payment context; results are not a canonical server quote or eligibility decision. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:17–27,142–163` | Hydrate all sensitive inputs from canonical records and return versioned quote/decision. |
| F-911 | P1 | Insurance service eligibility mismatch is emitted as a warning rather than rejection, while policy number/coverage/benefit verification is absent. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:50–67` | Verify payer benefits and fail closed for non-covered service. |
| F-912 | P1 | Eligibility fails open when service/patient fields are missing; no explicit required-context policy or patient consent/clinical validation exists. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:69–81` | Require complete context for restricted services and typed clinical eligibility. |
| F-913 | P1 | Provider acceptance warns on missing capability and permits missing provider/type context; provider hydration does not visibly check active status, license, facility scope or freshness. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:83–98,145–156` | Fail-closed verified provider capability/licence decision. |
| F-914 | P0 | Pricing trusts caller `service.price`, applies hard-coded 15% VAT and simplified 80% insurance discount without currency, tax source, policy version, quote expiry or ledger binding. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:100–122` | Canonical catalog/payer/tax quote with version, currency and expiry. |
| F-915 | P1 | Surge uses local JavaScript hour and inclusive end boundary, without timezone/holiday/override semantics; the same scheduled instant can price differently across runtimes. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:110–119` | Explicit service timezone and deterministic temporal policy. |
| F-916 | P1 | Pricing can calculate a final price while validation has warnings or missing context, with no explicit `price_is_authoritative`/blocking decision contract. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:29–39,101–122,142–163` | Separate blocking decision from quote and prohibit checkout on unresolved warnings. |
| F-917 | P1 | Payment-method policy allows missing method/context and is only a local allowlist, not a verified payment/insurance capability or settlement authorization. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:124–139` | Require explicit method/context and bind to live capability/settlement contract. |
| F-918 | P1 | Rule errors embed raw insurance provider and context values, potentially leaking input data and creating unstable client-facing error contracts. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:59–61,72–80,136–138` | Versioned sanitized error codes and localized message mapping. |
| F-919 | P1 | Provider hydration constructs capabilities from heterogeneous profile arrays without validating keys, normalization, status or source timestamp, so acceptance can be stale/inconsistent. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:145–156` | Normalized catalog capability projection with provenance. |
| F-920 | P1 | Business-rules controller exposes `config/surge` and `validate` under one JWT guard, with no visible role separation between policy mutation and caller validation. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:167–179` | Explicit admin-only config route and purpose-scoped validation API. |
| F-921 | P0 | Every domain is instructed to call `validate`, but no enforcement/adapter/version contract is visible; bypassing this module can create rule drift and inconsistent financial truth. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:2–7,142–163` | Enforce integration coverage and contract tests for every booking/transition path. |
| F-922 | P1 | Rule result exposes warnings/meta without schema constraints, allowing internal decision metadata or caller-influenced details to reach clients. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:29–39,142–163` | Typed redacted result projection and stable public contract. |
| F-923 | P1 | Surge/policy updates have no visible idempotency/concurrency control, so concurrent admin writes can overwrite each other and invalidate in-flight quotes. | `src/modules/business-rules/business-rules.module.ts:45–48,175–176` | Versioned compare-and-set policy updates and quote invalidation. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |

| F-1038 | P1 | No actor, reason, approval, change history or rollback metadata exists, preventing auditability of production feature activation. | `src/modules/feature-flags/feature-flag.schema.ts:4–10` | Immutable flag audit log and dual-control for high-risk features. |

| F-1041 | P1 | `key` has uniqueness but no visible normalization, namespace, format or reserved-prefix policy, allowing ambiguous or colliding semantic flags across modules. | `src/modules/feature-flags/feature-flag.schema.ts:6–7` | Namespaced normalized key registry and collision tests. |

| F-1049 | P1 | Product locale uses `fil` while `DbLang`/database mapping uses `tl`; without a canonical alias layer Filipino localization can silently miss or fall back incorrectly. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:10–19,162–170` | One canonical locale registry with explicit product↔DB alias tests. |
| F-1050 | P1 | Publication readiness checks names and only populated category/ingredient/form/strength fields, but does not require all display-critical structured fields or validate translation quality/completeness. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:21–47` | Locale-specific completeness policy and reviewed translation validation. |
| F-1051 | P0 | Heuristic Arabic-to-English fallback can translate clinical/catalog facts by regex substitution without source verification, allowing incorrect dosage-form, category, strength or package semantics. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:49–108,162–179` | Vetted translation/catalog source and clinical review for factual fields. |
| F-1052 | P1 | Global unit regexes are reused with `.test()` and `.replace()`; mutable `lastIndex` can make repeated localization calls order-dependent and nondeterministic. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:111–140` | Stateless regex handling and deterministic repeated-call tests. |
| F-1053 | P1 | Dosage/category/unit dictionaries are first-match heuristics with substring/ordering risks, so mixed Arabic text can be partially or incorrectly transformed while appearing localized. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:49–140` | Token-aware parser with ambiguity tests and unchanged-value reporting. |
| F-1054 | P1 | Urdu, Hindi, Bengali and Filipino values fall back to English dictionaries when source translations are absent, violating requested-language semantics while hiding missing translation status. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:156–179` | Locale-appropriate fallback policy and explicit untranslated markers/telemetry. |
| F-1055 | P1 | `localizeMedicineStructured` shallow-copies raw records and rewrites selected fields without provenance/version, clinical-vs-cosmetic classification or source confidence. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:143–179` | Field-level translation provenance/version and safe clinical-field policy. |
| F-1056 | P1 | The localization function does not validate `lang` against the public locale registry and can return raw/Arabic values silently for unsupported or unmapped requests. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:10–19,162–179` | Validated locale contract with explicit unsupported/untranslated outcome. |
| F-1057 | P1 | Translation readiness and runtime localization are separate mechanisms; a medicine may pass readiness while runtime output remains partially Arabic or English fallback, creating catalog/SEO parity drift. | `src/modules/medicines/med-i18n.ts:21–47,156–179` | Publication gate must use the same runtime projection and parity fixtures. |

## MailProcessor findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1118 | P0 | OTP destination and `secureCode` are accepted directly from unvalidated Bull job data; no schema, issuer, expiry or version validation is enforced. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:9–13` | Versioned validated job schema with bounded OTP lifetime and issuer contract. |
| F-1119 | P0 | The processor passes the OTP code directly to the mail service with no replay/deduplication identity or one-time delivery guarantee at this layer. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:9–13` | Idempotent delivery key and one-time/replay acceptance tests. |
| F-1120 | P0 | Error text from the mail provider is embedded in the thrown error and may be persisted in queue/DLQ telemetry, risking provider details or recipient-related PII leakage. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:13–17` | Redacted stable error codes; secrets/PII never enter logs or DLQ metadata. |
| F-1121 | P1 | All provider failures are treated identically; transient and permanent errors have no distinct retry/backoff/dead-letter policy in the processor contract. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:13–17` | Explicit failure taxonomy and bounded retry/backoff policy. |
| F-1122 | P1 | The comment claims direct DLQ payload routing, but the code only throws; actual DLQ configuration and retention are external and unproven here. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:15–16` | Verified queue/DLQ configuration, redaction and retention evidence. |
| F-1123 | P1 | No attempt-count, deadline or cancellation handling exists, so a delayed OTP job can send an expired code or retry beyond its useful security window. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:9–17` | Enforce expiry/deadline, attempts and cancellation before send. |
| F-1124 | P1 | No trace/audit metadata is attached to delivery success/failure, preventing reliable correlation from OTP issuance to email delivery outcome. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:9–18` | Correlation ID and privacy-safe delivery audit events. |
| F-1125 | P1 | No destination normalization or rate-limit linkage is enforced in the consumer, leaving abuse controls dependent on upstream callers and queue producers. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:9–13` | Normalized recipient contract and issuance/delivery abuse controls. |
| F-1126 | P2 | The processor assumes `sendOtp` result shape and queue semantics without a local contract assertion, allowing mail module/provider drift to fail late. | `src/modules/notifications/processors/mail.processor.ts:7–17` | Typed provider result contract and startup/contract tests. |

## Realtime SSE findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1127 | P0 | Booking SSE is explicitly public and filters only by caller-supplied `type`/`id`, with no authentication, capability or ownership check; this is a direct cross-user booking event disclosure/IDOR risk. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:27–36` | Require signed, scoped capability or owner/authorized-role check; stranger must receive 404/403 without event existence leakage. |
| F-1128 | P0 | Booking SSE emits raw event payloads and names without a public-safe allowlist or PII/clinical redaction. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:32–35` | Versioned public event DTO allowlist and redaction tests. |
| F-1129 | P1 | `type` and `id` path parameters are not validated or canonicalized, enabling ambiguous channel selection and malformed/high-cardinality subscriptions. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:28–34` | Strict enum/id validation, bounded length and rejected malformed identifiers. |
| F-1130 | P1 | Authenticated user stream trusts `user.id` typed as `any` and event `user_id` typed as `any`, without tenant/role scope or runtime event schema. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:15–23` | Typed authenticated principal and validated event envelope with tenant scope. |
| F-1131 | P1 | Both streams expose `Date.now()` connection/heartbeat data but no sequence, cursor or replay protocol, so reconnects can lose or duplicate security-relevant events. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:19–24,31–36` | Event IDs, Last-Event-ID replay policy and documented loss/duplication semantics. |
| F-1132 | P1 | No rate limit, connection cap, subscription TTL or per-client resource budget is enforced, enabling SSE connection and event-fanout exhaustion. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:16–36` | Per-user/IP/channel limits, TTL and load/abuse tests. |
| F-1133 | P1 | RxJS streams use `interval`/`fromEvent` without explicit teardown, filtering scope or subscriber accounting; disconnect and emitter-error cleanup behavior is unproven. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:19–24,31–36` | Verified unsubscribe/finalize cleanup, error handling and connection metrics. |
| F-1134 | P1 | Raw `any` casts for EventEmitter and event payload bypass compile-time contracts and permit producer-consumer schema drift. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:18–23,32–35` | Typed event map and runtime validation at the bridge boundary. |
| F-1135 | P1 | Anonymous SEO-tracking rationale conflicts with booking confidentiality; public stream semantics are not constrained to non-sensitive, non-personal events. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:27–30` | Separate public tracking channel from private booking channel with event classification. |
| F-1136 | P1 | No tenant isolation, role authorization or consent model is present for either event channel, allowing organizational/provider boundary mistakes. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:15–36` | Tenant/role/consent matrix and owner/provider stranger tests. |
| F-1137 | P1 | Module registration provides only the controller and does not evidence guards, interceptors, serializer/redaction, metrics or queue/event reliability configuration. | `src/modules/realtime/realtime.sse.ts:38–41` | Startup contract evidence for global policies plus end-to-end SSE security/reliability tests. |

| F-1149 | P1 | Application status update has only a value allowlist; it does not enforce legal transitions, actor role matrix, idempotency, compare-and-set or audit decorator. | `src/modules/recruitment/recruitment.module.ts:199–217,280–284` | State machine, authorized actor matrix, audit and idempotent mutation tests. |
| F-1150 | P1 | Public and authenticated job queries accept unvalidated filters and lack rate limits/limits, enabling query abuse and high-cardinality extraction. | `src/modules/recruitment/recruitment.module.ts:90–111,257–262` | Validated bounded filters, pagination, rate limits and query-cost controls. |
| F-1151 | P1 | No transaction/outbox is shown for application creation/status changes and downstream notifications, so persistence and messaging can diverge. | `src/modules/recruitment/recruitment.module.ts:151–159,214–216` | Transactional/outbox workflow with delivery/replay evidence. |

## Ratings module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1152 | P0 | Rating submission trusts client-supplied `provider_id` and entity identifiers without verifying that the caller owned/completed the referenced service or that provider matches the entity. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:17–24,32–44` | Resolve eligibility from canonical completed service and derive provider server-side. |
| F-1153 | P0 | Duplicate prevention is find-then-update/insert without unique index, transaction or idempotency; concurrent requests can create multiple ratings for one user/entity. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:25–44` | Unique compound index plus atomic upsert/idempotent replay tests. |
| F-1154 | P1 | Rating status is set directly to `published`; no moderation, abuse/spam, sensitive-content, edit-history or consent workflow exists. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:32–44` | Moderation state machine, content controls, audit and retention policy. |
| F-1155 | P0 | Public provider ratings return broad documents with `user_name` and free-text comment, with no privacy/minimum-necessary projection or redaction. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:60–77` | Public-safe DTO, identity policy and PII/sensitive-content tests. |
| F-1156 | P1 | `recompute` updates all profiles matching either `user_id` or `account_id` and runs outside the rating write, risking cross-profile updates and stale denormalized averages on partial failure/race. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:47–57` | Canonical provider identity, transactional/outbox recompute and consistency repair. |
| F-1157 | P1 | Public rating pagination parses untrusted strings without finite/range validation; negative/NaN page values and high offsets are not rejected. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:60–64,95–99` | Strict bounded pagination, query-cost limits and abuse tests. |
| F-1158 | P1 | No rate limit or anti-automation control is applied to public reads or rating writes, enabling enumeration, scraping or rating manipulation. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:85–104` | Per-user/IP/provider limits and anomaly detection. |
| F-1159 | P1 | Rating submit/update lacks idempotency and audit metadata, so client retries can alter comments/scores without a stable mutation record. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:25–44,89–93` | Idempotency-Key contract and immutable audit trail. |
| F-1160 | P1 | `mine` accepts arbitrary entity type/id without the same allowlist or eligibility checks as submit, leaving inconsistent ownership/contract semantics. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:80–82,101–105` | Shared identifier/entity validator and completed-service policy. |
| F-1161 | P1 | Rating fields and controller bodies use `any`; no runtime DTO validation constrains comment length/content, identifier format, score precision or provider identity. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:5,17,60,80,91–104` | Strict DTO/schema validation and bounded free-text contract. |
| F-1162 | P1 | Public rating aggregation and rows can diverge because average/count are independently recomputed and queried, with no snapshot/version consistency. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:47–77` | Versioned aggregate snapshot or read-model consistency contract. |
| F-1163 | P1 | No index/unique-index registration is shown for user/entity/provider keys or provider/status/time query paths, leaving correctness and performance dependent on external DB state. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:14–15,25,48–68` | Verified index manifest and startup/schema checks. |
| F-1164 | P1 | No event/outbox integration is shown for rating changes, provider aggregate updates, moderation or notifications, so downstream systems can miss or duplicate state. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:25–57` | Transactional event/outbox contract with replay/recovery tests. |
| F-1165 | P2 | Module registers only controller/service and does not evidence schema/index, guard, rate-limit, serializer or moderation configuration. | `src/modules/ratings/ratings.module.ts:108–112` | Explicit module dependencies and startup contract evidence. |

| F-1169 | P1 | No optimistic versioning or conflict handling is enforced for inventory records, allowing stale seed/runtime writes to overwrite changes. | `src/modules/seed/repositories/pharmacyinventory.repository.ts:8–13` | Versioned writes and conflict rejection. |
| F-1170 | P1 | No audit/provenance marker distinguishes seeded data from operational inventory changes, weakening rollback and forensic traceability. | `src/modules/seed/repositories/pharmacyinventory.repository.ts:8–13` | Seed run ID, source/version and immutable audit metadata. |
| F-1171 | P1 | No soft-delete/reconciliation lifecycle is represented, so rerunning seed data may leave stale inventory or fail to reconcile removed records. | `src/modules/seed/repositories/pharmacyinventory.repository.ts:8–13` | Idempotent seed reconciliation and explicit deactivation policy. |
| F-1172 | P1 | Generic inherited operations provide no field projection or secret/internal metadata redaction if this repository is reused by service callers. | `src/modules/seed/repositories/pharmacyinventory.repository.ts:8–13` | Separate internal seed model/projection from public inventory DTOs. |
| F-1173 | P2 | Non-functional import comment and formatting drift obscure repository ownership and seed/runtime separation. | `src/modules/seed/repositories/pharmacyinventory.repository.ts:4–6` | Remove stale comments and document repository provenance. |

| F-1256 | P1 | No environment gate or collection-level allowlist is encoded in the dataset, so high-impact identity/system/inventory fixtures can be imported without context. | `src/modules/seed/seed.data.ts:5–299` | Collection allowlist and hard environment policy. |
| F-1257 | P1 | No explicit distinction exists between synthetic test fixtures and production bootstrap records despite real-looking names, organizations and credentials. | `src/modules/seed/seed.data.ts:1–299` | Mandatory `synthetic:true`/fixture namespace and production rejection. |
| F-1258 | P1 | No retention, deletion/anonymization or secret rotation lifecycle is represented for seeded identity/provider data. | `src/modules/seed/seed.data.ts:5–299` | Automated cleanup, revocation and retention policy. |

## ProviderJobs module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1271 | P0 | Provider capability policy can fail open: generic `provider` and `admin` receive all domains, profile capability lookup errors are swallowed, and no assignment/facility/credential check is visible. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:55–80` | Explicit role/domain/facility/assignment policy; fail closed on capability lookup failure. |

| F-1274 | P1 | Queue output fabricates/obscures commercial truth with `total` fallback `0` and payment defaults when source fields are absent. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:121–140` | Explicit unknown/null semantics and server-authoritative commercial projection. |
| F-1275 | P1 | Combined queue has per-domain limits but no global pagination cursor or query validation; up to 250 records plus clinical enrichment can be returned per request. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:103–174` | Bounded global pagination/cursor and query-cost controls. |
| F-1276 | P0 | Generic action authorization accepts multiple roles but records `actor_role:'provider'` for all mutations, misrepresenting admin/pharmacy/lab actors in workflow history. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:188–210` | Derive actual actor role and enforce role/domain matrix. |
| F-1277 | P1 | Action transitions save after loading entity without visible compare-and-set/idempotency; concurrent accept/start/complete/replay can race or duplicate history. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:193–221` | Atomic transition with version/idempotency and replay tests. |
| F-1278 | P1 | A `Model` variable is selected inside the action mutation but never used, obscuring intended persistence boundary and contract review. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:210–215` | Remove dead selection or use explicit domain command repository. |
| F-1279 | P0 | `updateInsurance` spreads arbitrary client fields into `insurance_details` and trusts client `approvalStatus`, enabling unauthorized claim-state/data injection. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:228–245` | Strict insurance DTO, server-derived status, insurer/claim validation and field allowlist. |
| F-1280 | P0 | Insurance mutation has no patient consent, claim/amount/source validation, attachment verification, idempotency or audit decorator. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:228–250,263–267` | Consent/claim contract, verified attachments, idempotent audited workflow. |
| F-1281 | P1 | Controllers accept `any` query/body/principal values and arbitrary reason text; no DTO validation, bounded text or identifier validation is visible. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:254–267` | Strict DTOs, enum/id validation and bounded reason fields. |
| F-1282 | P1 | `my-capabilities` exposes role and derived capability set based on broad any principal/profile data without evidence of tenant/provider privacy policy. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:258–262` | Safe capability DTO and tenant/role disclosure policy. |
| F-1283 | P1 | Queue and action responses return `toObject()`/broad mapped values without a shared serializer, risking internal booking, address, contact, attachment or insurance leakage. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:121–140,216–220,228–250` | Versioned domain-specific public/provider DTOs and redaction tests. |
| F-1284 | P1 | No rate limits, abuse detection or audit metrics are wired for provider queue enumeration or repeated actions. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:254–267,270–288` | Per-provider/domain limits, audit metrics and anomaly controls. |
| F-1285 | P1 | No transaction/outbox boundary is visible between booking state save, state history and downstream notifications/events. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:207–221` | Transactional state/event publication with recovery and replay tests. |
| F-1286 | P1 | Model registrations mix string names and `Appointment.name`, with no central token/index/schema readiness assertion for this cross-domain module. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:27–35,273–282` | Canonical model registry and startup schema/index checks. |
| F-1287 | P1 | Module includes operational patient/provider/user/attachment models but no visible redaction, tenant guard, serializer, rate-limit or policy provider wiring. | `src/modules/provider-jobs/provider-jobs.module.ts:270–288` | Explicit security dependencies and startup contract evidence. |
