## Main bootstrap findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1289 | P1 | Sentry initializes with an empty DSN fallback and no visible PII scrubbing policy at bootstrap; production observability can be silently absent or leak sensitive context through downstream filters. | `src/main.ts:1–6,86–87` | Fail/alert on missing production DSN and enforce tested PII scrubbing. |
| F-1290 | P0 | In-memory Mongo startup failure is caught and the process continues after `USE_MEMORY_MONGO=true`, risking an application booting without the intended database rather than failing closed. | `src/main.ts:27–42` | Fail closed on requested memory DB failure and expose explicit non-production status. |
| F-1291 | P0 | Missing `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` becomes `allowedOrigins=true` outside production, enabling broad CORS in any non-production environment that may still contain sensitive data or be externally reachable. | `src/main.ts:44–55` | Explicit allowlist or hard fail for every externally reachable environment. |
| F-1292 | P1 | CORS origin/method policy is configured globally without visible origin normalization, environment classification, preflight audit or per-route sensitivity policy. | `src/main.ts:44–55` | Validated origin registry and environment-specific CORS tests. |
| F-1293 | P1 | Production CSP permits `'unsafe-inline'` for script and style and allows all HTTPS image sources, weakening XSS/content-origin controls. | `src/main.ts:70–81` | Nonce/hash-based CSP and restricted asset origins with exception review. |
| F-1294 | P1 | `crossOriginEmbedderPolicy` is disabled globally without a documented route/asset reason or compensating control. | `src/main.ts:70–81` | Explicit reviewed exception or enablement where compatible. |
| F-1295 | P0 | Global 25MB JSON/urlencoded limits and complete raw-body capture copy request contents into memory, increasing request-amplification, PII-retention and sensitive-upload exposure. | `src/main.ts:89–96` | Route-specific limits, bounded raw-body use, redaction and upload streaming. |
| F-1296 | P1 | Raw body is attached as `req.rawBody` without visible cleanup, size accounting, content-type restriction or downstream logging/redaction contract. | `src/main.ts:89–95` | Explicit raw-body lifecycle and sensitive-content tests. |
| F-1297 | P1 | Trust proxy is hardcoded to two hops; topology changes or direct exposure can make client IP/rate-limit/audit attribution incorrect. | `src/main.ts:64–68` | Deployment-validated proxy chain and spoofing tests. |
| F-1298 | P1 | Swagger is enabled by default in every non-production environment and can be enabled in production without visible authentication/IP restriction in bootstrap. | `src/main.ts:110–119` | Explicit documentation policy, protected access and exposure tests. |
| F-1299 | P1 | Startup performs no visible required-config completeness, external dependency readiness, timeout or health-gate validation before listening. | `src/main.ts:121–129` | Fail-closed startup checks plus liveness/readiness separation. |
| F-1300 | P1 | Shutdown hooks are enabled but no visible drain deadline, in-flight request/queue coordination or readiness withdrawal is configured. | `src/main.ts:121–124` | Bounded graceful drain and readiness transition tests. |
| F-1301 | P2 | Bootstrap uses `console.log/error` for memory Mongo lifecycle alongside Winston, creating inconsistent structured logging and possible URI disclosure. | `src/main.ts:33–40` | Structured redacted logging only. |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| F-1305 | P1 | `InsuranceEngineModule` is imported twice, creating duplicate registration/drift risk for providers, handlers or scheduled effects. | `src/app.module.ts:127,251` | Single canonical registration with duplicate-module test. |
| F-1306 | P1 | Root imports overlapping compatibility/admin/provider/insurance modules without an explicit route ownership registry, risking duplicate or shadowed contracts. | `src/app.module.ts:125–127,200–256` | Canonical route ownership and collision/startup assertions. |
| F-1307 | P0 | Global JwtAuthGuard and RolesGuard are installed but root wiring does not show public-route exceptions, tenant scope or policy ordering; fail-open/incorrect ordering cannot be ruled out here. | `src/app.module.ts:258–261` | Explicit global policy matrix and unauth/owner/stranger tests. |
| F-1308 | P1 | Global ThrottlerGuard defaults to 200 requests/minute without sensitivity-specific partitions or root-visible trust-boundary configuration. | `src/app.module.ts:169–171,258–261` | Endpoint/class/user/IP-aware limits with proxy/spoofing tests. |
| F-1309 | P1 | Global AuditLogInterceptor has no root-visible redaction, retention, actor/tenant context or failure policy. | `src/app.module.ts:262–264` | Audited redaction/retention and fail-safe logging contract. |
| F-1310 | P1 | Global IdempotencyInterceptor has no root-visible storage/TTL/key scope or coverage policy, so replay safety for every mutation is not proven by composition alone. | `src/app.module.ts:262–264` | Explicit idempotency contract, persistence and mutation coverage tests. |
| F-1311 | P1 | Global middleware applies BansMiddleware and CorrelationMiddleware to `*` without root-visible ordering, exclusion, error or fail-closed semantics. | `src/app.module.ts:266–269` | Middleware ordering/exclusion/error contract and tests. |
| F-1312 | P1 | Root registers HealthController, HealthDashboardController and ProviderPayoutsController without visible exposure/role declarations at composition point, increasing public/admin misrouting risk. | `src/app.module.ts:257` | Explicit controller exposure matrix and unauthenticated access tests. |
| F-1313 | P1 | Global Mongoose audit plugin is installed without root-visible schema exclusion/redaction or PII/secret policy. | `src/app.module.ts:156–164` | Audit plugin redaction and sensitive-collection tests. |
| F-1314 | P1 | EventEmitterModule and ScheduleModule are globally enabled with no root-level ownership/duplication/exception registry for handlers and cron jobs. | `src/app.module.ts:169–170` | Handler/schedule inventory and duplicate-effect tests. |
| F-1315 | P1 | Root wiring includes Webhooks, Realtime, RealtimeSse, Push, Chat and LiveKit surfaces together without visible origin/channel/tenant isolation at composition level. | `src/app.module.ts:139–140,183,224,227,229` | Explicit channel ownership, origin and tenant isolation matrix. |
| F-1316 | P1 | SeedModule is loaded in the same root runtime as production modules, with no visible build/runtime condition preventing accidental bootstrap exposure. | `src/app.module.ts:173–185` | Conditional non-production registration or capability hard stop. |
| F-1317 | P2 | Root file has imports interleaved with executable helper placement and a very large compressed module array, reducing auditability and increasing wiring drift risk. | `src/app.module.ts:2–133,135–256` | Structured module registry, formatting/lint and route-collision checks. |

## Root HealthController findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1318 | P1 | Public root health response discloses application identity and fixed version string, creating unnecessary fingerprinting and stale-version risk. | `src/health.controller.ts:14–22` | Minimal public response or protected diagnostic metadata with build provenance. |
| F-1319 | P1 | Liveness always returns `up` without event-loop/process health checks, so a wedged process can remain eligible for traffic. | `src/health.controller.ts:25–29` | Liveness contract with event-loop/process failure behavior. |
| F-1320 | P0 | Readiness returns HTTP success with body status `degraded` when Mongo or Redis is down, allowing load balancers/orchestrators to treat an unready instance as ready. | `src/health.controller.ts:31–57` | Return non-2xx on readiness failure and test dependency outages. |
| F-1321 | P1 | Redis readiness ping has no timeout/deadline, so a hung Redis client can hang health checks and deployment decisions. | `src/health.controller.ts:41–44` | Bounded probe timeout and deterministic failure response. |
| F-1322 | P1 | Public readiness exposes Mongo/Redis dependency topology and up/down state to unauthenticated callers. | `src/health.controller.ts:31–56` | Minimal public readiness or protected detailed diagnostics. |
| F-1323 | P1 | Readiness checks only Mongo connection state and Redis ping; queue workers, critical integrations, startup grace, cache and payment dependencies are not represented. | `src/health.controller.ts:33–56` | Dependency-specific readiness matrix with criticality policy. |
| F-1324 | P1 | Empty catches suppress diagnostic context for Mongo/Redis failures, weakening alerting and incident evidence. | `src/health.controller.ts:37–44` | Structured redacted error metrics/logging without response leakage. |
| F-1325 | P2 | Health response has no build commit/schema/config version, making stale deployment detection and rollback verification harder. | `src/health.controller.ts:46–57` | Immutable build/version metadata in protected diagnostics. |

## Insurance-company seed script findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1326 | P0 | Script connects directly to Mongo and has no production/environment hard stop or database identity assertion; a manual invocation can write the wrong database. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:21–25` | Environment allowlist, database identity assertion and production rejection. |
| F-1327 | P0 | `DB_NAME` silently defaults to `nabd`, creating wrong-database write risk when deployment configuration is missing. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:21–25` | Required DB_NAME in controlled environments; no implicit fallback. |
| F-1328 | P0 | Claimed idempotency is non-atomic check-then-insert; concurrent runs can duplicate companies unless an externally created unique index exists and is verified. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:27–32` | Atomic upsert/unique index and concurrent replay tests. |
| F-1329 | P1 | Script neither creates nor verifies a unique index on company code, so its duplicate protection is not self-proving. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:25–32` | Startup index assertion and duplicate rejection. |
| F-1330 | P1 | Ten insurer names/codes are hardcoded without regulator/source provenance, license status, freshness, locale version or approval metadata. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:8–19` | Canonical verified source, version and approval gate. |
| F-1331 | P1 | Inserts always set `is_active:true` with no verification or effective-date policy, allowing stale/unsupported insurers to appear active. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:31` | Server-controlled lifecycle and source-effective status. |
| F-1332 | P1 | Random UUID IDs are generated on each insertion and no deterministic source key/version/reconciliation metadata is persisted. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:5–6,28–32` | Stable source key and versioned idempotent reconciliation. |
| F-1333 | P1 | Raw collection access bypasses application schema/DTO/validation/policy layers. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:24–31` | Shared typed schema/validator or explicitly governed migration path. |
| F-1334 | P1 | No dry-run, batch/lock, operator authorization, audit run ID or rollback mechanism is present. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:21–35` | Audited bounded command with approval, dry-run and rollback. |
| F-1335 | P1 | Existing records are skipped but stale/renamed/removed companies are never reconciled or deactivated. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:27–32` | Full desired-state reconciliation with deactivation policy. |
| F-1336 | P1 | Failure path logs only to console, exits without a visible `finally` disconnect, and provides no structured/redacted incident evidence. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:21–24,36–38` | Guaranteed cleanup and structured redacted operational logging. |
| F-1337 | P2 | Documentation says `npx ts-node` and labels the script idempotent but does not document permissions, source version or operational safety prerequisites. | `src/scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:1–4` | Accurate runbook with preflight and safety constraints. |

## Production verification script findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1351 | P0 | Verification requires `MONGO_URI` while application wiring consumes `MONGO_URL`, so the gate can reject a valid deployment or verify a variable the app does not use. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:12–24`; `src/app.module.ts:156–159` | Single canonical env contract with parity test. |
| F-1352 | P0 | “Comprehensive” verification checks only five env-variable presences plus Mongo state and Redis ping; it does not prove values are valid, scoped, reachable or used by enabled integrations. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:12–38` | Capability-specific validation matrix with actionable checks. |
| F-1353 | P0 | Script creates the full `AppModule` context, loading seed/legacy/compatibility and scheduled/operational modules during verification, risking side effects against production. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:24`; `src/app.module.ts:135–256` | Side-effect-free verification graph or explicit disabled-runtime mode. |
| F-1354 | P1 | Redis ping has no timeout/deadline, so a hung Redis client can hang release verification. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:32–36` | Bounded probes and deterministic timeout failure. |
| F-1355 | P1 | Mongo check relies only on global connection `readyState`; it does not verify selected database identity, TLS, auth, indexes, migrations or read/write permissions. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:26–30` | Database identity, security, schema/index and permission checks. |
| F-1356 | P1 | Payment and AI env variables are presence-checked but no live contract, credential scope, webhook, idempotency or safe non-financial capability test is performed. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:12–22` | Integration verification without real charges, with webhook/idempotency proof. |
| F-1357 | P1 | Success message declares the entire system production-ready after only infrastructure probes, creating an unsafe false GO signal. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:40–45` | Evidence-based report with explicit PASS/BLOCKED/NOT-TESTED states. |
| F-1358 | P1 | No HTTP listener/route, auth/cookie, CORS/CSP, storage, mail/SMS, websocket, webhook, health-status or rollback verification is performed. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:24–45` | Contract and deployment smoke matrix. |
| F-1359 | P1 | No verification of absence of seed/mock/placeholder data or accidental production seed exposure is performed. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:24–45` | Source/runtime data integrity and seed exclusion checks. |
| F-1360 | P1 | Failure path exits without a visible `finally` closing the application context, potentially leaving connections/resources open before forced process termination. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:24,46–49` | Guaranteed cleanup and bounded exit. |
| F-1361 | P1 | Error output logs only `error.message`, losing check-level evidence, error classification and structured redacted diagnostics. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:46–49` | Structured verification artifact with redaction and exit taxonomy. |
| F-1362 | P1 | No lock/once-only/bounded execution or concurrency control prevents multiple verification contexts from stressing production dependencies. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:7–49` | Bounded, observable verification command. |
| F-1363 | P1 | Required env list includes `GEMINI_API_KEY` and `MOYASAR_API_KEY` without proving those names match the actual integration configuration, allowing configuration drift. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:12–22` | Generated env contract from integration registry. |
| F-1364 | P2 | Runbook calls the script “comprehensive” and “fully verified” without documenting its narrow scope or untested production surfaces. | `src/scripts/verify-production.ts:7–13,40–45` | Accurate runbook with limitations and conditional release decision. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| F-1366 | P1 | Draft interfaces provide no runtime validation/decorators, so scope, purpose, actor role, dates, coordinates and status can be malformed at HTTP boundaries if reused directly. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:21–60` | Runtime DTO/schema validation and bounded enums/ranges. |
| F-1367 | P0 | Consent draft has no subject/actor ownership, participant authorization, purpose binding, version conflict, consent evidence or server-side state transition implementation. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:21–35` | Consent state machine with owner/participant/purpose/version/audit tests. |
| F-1368 | P1 | `actor_role`, `purpose`, `version` and `source` are unconstrained strings, enabling ambiguous or forged consent semantics if accepted as input. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:24–34` | Controlled registries and server-derived actor/purpose/source metadata. |
| F-1369 | P0 | QR draft contains signature/security claims but no algorithm, key retrieval/rotation, issuer/audience/purpose binding, clock skew or signature verification implementation. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:37–50` | Cryptographic verification contract with key rotation and negative tests. |
| F-1370 | P0 | QR draft has no persistent nonce/JTI replay protection or resource ownership/participant check. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:43–49` | Atomic replay store and owner/participant/resource binding tests. |
| F-1371 | P1 | Emergency location draft lacks coordinate bounds, accuracy policy, privacy granularity guarantee, retention/deletion, access audit and emergency lifecycle. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:52–60` | Privacy-preserving bounded location contract with retention and audit. |
| F-1372 | P1 | Consent/status dates are unconstrained strings and no ordering/expiry/revocation consistency is enforced. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:19,28–32` | ISO date validation and state/date invariant tests. |
| F-1373 | P1 | Draft error registry is not proven aligned with global exception filters/OpenAPI/status semantics and may diverge from live error codes. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:62–72` | Generated registry parity and HTTP contract tests. |
| F-1374 | P1 | `assertPhase6ContractInactive` only throws when explicitly called and does not make the underlying routes/callers impossible to activate accidentally. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:74–83` | Route-level feature gate and startup assertion that inactive contracts have no handlers. |
| F-1375 | P1 | `assertPhase6ContractInactive` accepts every registry key although only three are inactive contract cases; this can blur error taxonomy and misuse the helper. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:62–83` | Narrow inactive-contract type and explicit error mapping. |
| F-1376 | P1 | No persistence/schema/audit/retention model is defined for consent, QR events or emergency location, preventing evidence-backed lifecycle and deletion guarantees. | `src/contracts/phase6-contracts.ts:21–60` | Versioned schemas, audit, retention and deletion contract. |

| F-1469 | P1 | No recording/telemetry privacy, consent or media-retention boundary is represented at the repository level. | `src/modules/livekit/repositories/callsession.repository.ts:8–13` | Recording consent, media policy and retention tests. |
| F-1470 | P1 | No audit/provenance records who created, joined, ended, revoked or accessed a call session and under which appointment purpose. | `src/modules/livekit/repositories/callsession.repository.ts:8–13` | Immutable session access/change audit. |
| F-1471 | P1 | Generic inherited operations expose no schema-level validation of room names, participant IDs, timestamps, status or token claims in this member. | `src/modules/livekit/repositories/callsession.repository.ts:8–13` | Typed validation and token/session invariant tests. |
| F-1472 | P2 | Non-functional import comment and formatting drift obscure ownership of this security-sensitive realtime repository. | `src/modules/livekit/repositories/callsession.repository.ts:4–6` | Remove stale comment and document session privacy boundary. |

## Doctors schemas findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1483 | P1 | Doctor commercial/public fields use unconstrained primitives and zero/default values, allowing unverified fees, rating, review counts and availability to become visible truth. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:14–27,32–39` | Server-authoritative validated projections and approval/source gates. |
| F-1484 | P1 | Doctor `insurance_supported`, `clinic_location`, images, tags, weekly schedule and blocked dates use free-form arrays/objects without visible nested validation or provenance. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:27–36` | Typed nested schemas, geographic/time validation and source audit. |
| F-1485 | P1 | Doctor status is a free string despite a comment describing a three-value enum; accepting/online/clinic flags default true, risking premature public availability. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:23–39` | Runtime enum and explicit publication/readiness workflow. |
| F-1486 | P1 | Slug is generated from mutable names and UUID in a save hook, with no visible collision/rewrite policy or stable URL lifecycle. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:44–49` | Stable canonical slug, collision handling and redirect/deprecation policy. |
| F-1487 | P0 | Doctor appointments persist patient name and phone directly with no schema-level redaction/projection or access policy. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:57–61` | Minimum-necessary DTOs and owner/participant access tests. |
| F-1488 | P0 | Appointment `type`, payment method and insurance status are free strings despite TypeScript aliases, enabling invalid or ambiguous booking/payment states. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:52–69` | Runtime enums and server-derived payment/insurance state machine. |
| F-1489 | P0 | Appointment fee defaults to zero and is stored without currency, price-version, tax or server-calculation provenance. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:63–69` | Server-authoritative priced quote with currency/version/tax source. |
| F-1490 | P0 | Doctor/time index is non-unique and no visible slot-capacity/overlap invariant is encoded, so concurrent appointments can oversell a slot. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:75–77` | Atomic slot lock/unique capacity enforcement and race tests. |
| F-1491 | P1 | Appointment documents, address and state history are `any`/free-form arrays, risking unsafe attachment/address data and untrusted history injection. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:70–73` | Typed nested schemas, allowlists, immutable server history and redaction. |
| F-1492 | P1 | Appointment state is a TypeScript union but no schema enum or legal-transition guard is visible. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:52–65` | Runtime enum and guarded transition matrix. |
| F-1493 | P1 | Doctor chat messages lack appointment participant/tenant constraint, sender-role enum, text bounds, content moderation and attachment validation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:79–88` | Participant-scoped chat contract, bounded content and safe attachment schema. |
| F-1494 | P0 | Consultation notes persist diagnosis, notes, follow-up and prescriptions without schema-level clinical access, signing/finalization, amendment audit or validation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:90–101` | Signed clinical-note lifecycle, role/patient access, validation and audit. |
| F-1495 | P1 | Prescription subdocuments use free-form medicine/dose/duration strings without medicine identity, dose/unit, duration or interaction provenance. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:96–99` | Canonical medicine references and validated prescription schema. |
| F-1496 | P1 | Notification recipient role/type/entity/deep link/body are free strings with no URL allowlist, template or tenant validation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:103–117` | Typed notification templates, safe links and recipient scope tests. |
| F-1497 | P1 | Notification schema has no deterministic deduplication/idempotency key; repeated events can produce duplicate user alerts. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:103–117` | Event-derived unique key and replay tests. |
| F-1498 | P1 | Notification read state has no version/conflict or actor policy, so concurrent clients can lose updates or mark another account's notification read if callers mis-scope. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:106–117` | Recipient-scoped atomic read mutation and conflict tests. |
| F-1499 | P1 | Ratings/reviews, availability, fees, insurance support and clinic location are stored alongside profile identity without visible verification/source/effective-date separation. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:12–39` | Separate verified projections with source/effective-date metadata. |
| F-1500 | P2 | Schema comments and formatting imply constraints that are not enforced at runtime, reducing contract auditability. | `src/modules/doctors/doctors.schemas.ts:36,39,52–53` | Runtime schema parity checks and lint/documentation cleanup. |

## Legal module findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1501 | P1 | Public policy retrieval exposes version, effective date, last update, change log and applicability without visible publication/redaction policy. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:48–66,160–168` | Public projection and publication review contract. |
| F-1502 | P1 | Language handling treats every non-Arabic language as English and falls back to Arabic, so requested locales can receive misleading legal content without explicit locale status. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:48–61` | Supported-locale contract and explicit fallback disclosure. |
| F-1503 | P0 | Policy upsert accepts an untyped arbitrary patch and spreads fields into storage, allowing unapproved/unknown legal fields or content to be written. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:68–95,180–185` | Strict DTO/field allowlist and legal review gate. |
| F-1504 | P1 | Policy version is auto-incremented only as a minor number and effective date/content/acceptance applicability have no visible monotonicity or publication invariant. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:68–95` | Versioned immutable publication lifecycle and conflict tests. |
| F-1505 | P0 | Legal acceptance uses non-atomic find-then-insert; concurrent requests can duplicate acceptances unless a unique compound index exists and is verified. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:98–112` | Atomic unique acceptance key and replay/concurrency tests. |
| F-1506 | P0 | Acceptance inserts before enterprise snapshot; snapshot failure can leave an accepted state without the legally traceable archive promised by the response. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:103–120` | Transaction/outbox/compensation and failure-path tests. |
| F-1507 | P1 | Legal acceptance persists IP, device ID, user-agent and client-derived platform with no visible minimization, validation, retention or user disclosure policy. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:103–119` | PII minimization, retention and provenance policy. |
| F-1508 | P1 | Acceptance metadata trusts arbitrary `x-device-id` and `x-app-platform` headers and infers platform from user-agent, making evidence spoofable. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:108–118` | Server/device attestation policy and bounded metadata. |
| F-1509 | P1 | Acceptance route has no visible Idempotency-Key requirement despite creating legal and archive records. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:174–178` | Idempotent consent contract and replay tests. |
| F-1510 | P1 | Pending acceptance query loads all user acceptances and uses broad `any` applicability values with permissive `provider`/`patient` inclusion, risking incorrect obligations and query cost. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:123–128` | Typed applicability registry, bounded query and policy tests. |
| F-1511 | P0 | Finance config lazy initialization inserts hardcoded commission/VAT/payout defaults without unique-index, currency/effective-date, approval or environment/source controls. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:16–28,41–46` | Canonical finance configuration with approval, source and atomic initialization. |
| F-1512 | P0 | Commission update spreads arbitrary admin patch fields into finance configuration without field allowlist, percent/range/tax validation or optimistic versioning. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:137–145,192–197` | Strict finance DTO, bounds, version and audit-before/after transaction. |
| F-1513 | P0 | Commission config is updated before history recording; history/audit failure can leave financially material changes without durable audit. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:137–145` | Transactional config/history update or outbox with recovery. |
| F-1514 | P1 | `commissionFor` returns 10% for unknown service types, a silent financial fallback that can misprice provider payouts. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:147–153,199–203` | Reject unknown service types or require explicit approved default. |
| F-1515 | P1 | `commissionFor` accepts arbitrary provider/service identifiers without tenant/provider status, currency or effective-date validation. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:147–153` | Canonical service/provider registry and effective-dated lookup. |
| F-1516 | P1 | Finance commission lookup is authenticated but not visibly restricted to a role/tenant, while the endpoint returns provider-specific override truth. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:199–203` | Explicit finance-reader role and tenant/provider disclosure policy. |
| F-1517 | P1 | Raw Mongo collections and `any` responses/bodies bypass schema validation, redaction and API/OpenAPI parity for legal and financial records. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:37–45,48–153,183–203` | Typed repositories/DTOs and generated contract parity. |
| F-1518 | P2 | Legal module combines public policy, consent evidence, admin policy editing and financial commission controls, increasing blast radius without visible bounded module separation. | `src/modules/legal/legal.module.ts:156–211` | Separate policy/consent/finance boundaries and authorization matrices. |

## MetricsInterceptor findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1519 | P1 | Path normalization falls back to `req.url` and does not visibly remove query strings, so user-controlled query values can enter Redis hash fields and increase cardinality/PII risk. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:15–21,26–29` | Use route templates, strip query/fragment, bound and validate labels. |
| F-1520 | P1 | Normalizer only recognizes selected UUID/ObjectId/long numeric patterns; arbitrary identifiers, encoded values and malicious long paths can remain high-cardinality despite truncation. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:16–21` | Canonical route template mapping with bounded label registry. |
| F-1521 | P1 | Metrics omit HTTP method, tenant, actor, status reason and correlation ID, making admin analytics ambiguous and cross-tenant aggregation unsafe. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:24–31,37–52` | Explicit dimensions with PII-safe tenant/actor pseudonyms and access policy. |
| F-1522 | P1 | Sensitive endpoint paths are aggregated without a redaction/category policy, risking inference of patient, admin, payment or clinical operations from telemetry. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:28–41` | Sensitive-route categories and redacted labels. |
| F-1523 | P0 | Redis telemetry failures are silently swallowed, so loss of operational evidence is invisible and monitoring can falsely appear healthy. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:33–45` | Failure metric/alert, bounded retry and explicit telemetry health state. |
| F-1524 | P1 | Redis acquisition and `multi/exec` calls have no timeout, retry budget, backpressure or memory/cardinality control; a degraded Redis can amplify request latency/resource use. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:33–45` | Non-blocking bounded telemetry path with timeout and resource budgets. |
| F-1525 | P1 | Pipeline errors are ignored without checking per-command results, so partial increments/expiry failures can corrupt request and status totals. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:38–44` | Interpret pipeline results and reconcile or alert on partial failure. |
| F-1526 | P1 | Retention is set on daily keys only when a request records; abandoned/stale keys and key-count/cardinality growth have no cleanup or budget policy. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:29–44` | Explicit retention sweep, cardinality limits and memory monitoring. |
| F-1527 | P1 | Status classification defaults all successful interceptor completions to `res.statusCode || 200` and errors to `err.status/statusCode || 500`, without handling redirects, cancellations or custom error semantics. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:37–52` | Defined status taxonomy and edge-case tests. |
| F-1528 | P1 | Global telemetry executes after response completion/error through RxJS tap but has no guarantee for streams, aborted clients or headers-sent paths. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:48–52` | Request lifecycle instrumentation covering abort/stream/timeout cases. |
| F-1529 | P1 | Redis client access uses `(this.redis as any).getClient?.()`, bypassing compile-time API guarantees and allowing silent no-op when the client contract changes. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:33–36` | Typed metrics client contract and startup/health assertion. |
| F-1530 | P2 | Comment promises “every API call” and 14-day telemetry but the implementation bypasses non-HTTP traffic and has no evidence artifact/coverage assertion. | `src/modules/ops/metrics.interceptor.ts:5–10,24–25` | Accurate documentation and coverage/telemetry contract tests. |

| F-1567 | P1 | Fixed durations and free-form tags/specialties have no runtime validation or capacity/availability semantics. | `src/modules/home-care/home-care.seed.ts:7–12,18–23,29–34,40–45,53–58,64–69,75–80,86–91,99–104,110–115,121–126,132–137` | Typed catalog schema with duration, specialty and availability constraints. |

## HomeCareBooking schema findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1578 | P0 | HomeCareBooking has no explicit booking ID, request/idempotency key or visible uniqueness/index contract for a multi-date booking. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:14–23` | Stable booking identity, idempotency and unique/index contract. |
| F-1579 | P0 | Patient, nurse and service IDs are required strings but have no schema-level relationship/tenant verification or owner/participant boundary. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:15–18` | Canonical foreign-key/tenant validation and owner/participant tests. |
| F-1580 | P0 | Selected dates, time and frequency are free-form with no timezone, date-range, recurrence, overlap or service-capacity validation. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:20–23` | Typed schedule contract, timezone and atomic availability tests. |
| F-1581 | P0 | No visible uniqueness/capacity invariant prevents concurrent booking of the same nurse/date/time or multi-date overlap. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:14–23,35–36` | Compound unique/slot-lock capacity enforcement and race tests. |
| F-1582 | P0 | Patient location stores address and coordinates as a free-form object without geospatial bounds, minimum-necessary projection, serviceability or retention policy. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:25–27` | Typed location schema, geofence/serviceability and PII policy. |
| F-1583 | P0 | Booking status omits cancelled, rejected, expired, rescheduled, no-show, failed-payment and disputed states, making lifecycle truth incomplete. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:4–12,29–31` | Complete guarded booking/payment state machine. |
| F-1584 | P0 | Status enum does not itself enforce legal transitions, actor permissions, timestamps or transition provenance. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:29–31` | Transition command layer with actor/time/audit invariants. |
| F-1585 | P0 | `total_amount` and `transport_fee` are optional unconstrained numbers with no currency, tax, quote/version, server calculation or price provenance. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:29–34` | Server-authoritative quote, currency/tax/version and payment reconciliation. |
| F-1586 | P0 | Payment method is enum-limited but no payment intent, authorization, capture/refund or failed-payment linkage is represented in the schema. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:29–34` | Payment state/linkage contract and replay/settlement tests. |
| F-1587 | P0 | Insurance details persist provider, policy number and coverage status as a free-form object without redaction, authorization, evidence or lifecycle policy. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:30–34` | Typed insurance contract, coverage verification and sensitive-field projection. |
| F-1588 | P1 | No nurse credential/specialty, service clinical eligibility, equipment or prescription/order binding is encoded for home-care procedures. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:15–18,29–34` | Clinical eligibility/credential/equipment gates. |
| F-1589 | P1 | No optimistic versioning or idempotent command boundary prevents concurrent reschedule, status or payment updates from overwriting each other. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:14–35` | Versioned commands, idempotency and conflict tests. |
| F-1590 | P1 | No retention, deletion, anonymization or legal-hold lifecycle is defined for location, insurance and booking data. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:14–35` | Retention/deletion/anonymization contract and tests. |
| F-1591 | P1 | No immutable audit/provenance fields record who created/changed/confirmed/cancelled the booking, source, consent or quote. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:14–35` | Immutable booking/payment/coverage audit. |
| F-1592 | P2 | The schema uses free-form `string` fields despite enums/types and comments, creating runtime contract drift and weak OpenAPI/schema parity. | `src/modules/home-care/schemas/home-care-booking.schema.ts:21–34` | Runtime enum/nested schema parity and generated contract checks. |

## Seed loyalty script findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1761 | P0 | Loyalty seed script has a localhost Mongo fallback and no production/database identity gate, so accidental execution can mutate the wrong database. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:8–10,113–121` | Fail-closed environment gate and explicit target confirmation. |
| F-1762 | P0 | Script is executable via direct ts-node and has no operator authorization, dry-run, audit actor or provenance requirement. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:2–6,113–138` | Authorized, auditable, dry-run-capable seed command. |
| F-1763 | P0 | Challenges and rewards are hardcoded catalog truth with no external approval/source/version/effective-date governance. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:18–111` | Reviewed versioned catalog source and approval gate. |
| F-1764 | P0 | Cashback/coupon values are hardcoded without currency/tax/ledger/settlement or redemption reconciliation. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:66–110` | Server-authoritative reward valuation and ledger reconciliation. |
| F-1765 | P0 | Every reward is unconditionally assigned `stock: 999`, which is a placeholder-like finite inventory rather than a verified inventory source. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:124–129` | Real inventory/quota source and reservation/release semantics. |
| F-1766 | P1 | Reruns overwrite active/date fields and reset reward stock without version or operator review, potentially reviving or changing already-published catalog truth. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:117–129` | Idempotent reconciliation preserving approved state and version conflicts. |
| F-1767 | P1 | Upsert identity depends only on short application IDs and no visible unique-index/reconciliation assertion is performed. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:117–129` | Unique index verification and deterministic import reconciliation. |
| F-1768 | P1 | Challenge period is calculated as execution time plus 90 days, with no business season, timezone, approval or historical immutability. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:12–13,120` | Effective-dated campaign lifecycle with timezone and approval. |
| F-1769 | P1 | Challenge and reward definitions lack redemption limits, per-user eligibility, expiration, revocation, abuse controls or idempotent redemption linkage. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:18–111` | Eligibility/redemption policy, replay protection and revocation. |
| F-1770 | P1 | Loyalty reward catalog is not atomically coupled to points ledger, coupon issuance or wallet cashback settlement. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:117–129` | Transactional/outbox reward issuance and ledger reconciliation. |
| F-1771 | P1 | Descriptions and challenge triggers are hardcoded strings with no locale/version/clinical/commercial review metadata. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:20–62,68–109` | Reviewed localized catalog and content provenance. |
| F-1772 | P1 | No validation enforces positive counts/points/values, supported award reasons, reward-type/value compatibility or safe stock bounds. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:15–17,18–111,124–129` | Typed schema validation and invariant tests. |
| F-1773 | P1 | Active-document counts are only logged and do not reconcile expected IDs, duplicates, dates, stock or catalog completeness. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:132–134` | Deterministic post-seed reconciliation with fail-closed result. |
| F-1774 | P1 | Mongo disconnect is only on success; error handling logs and exits without guaranteed cleanup or structured operational result. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:113–138` | Finally-based cleanup and structured failure/audit reporting. |
| F-1775 | P2 | `@ts-nocheck` disables compile-time safety for a script mutating loyalty and financial-adjacent collections. | `scripts/seed-loyalty.ts:1` | Strict typing and CI/static checks for seed scripts. |

## Deployment script findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1776 | P0 | `ENV` accepts prod/staging labels but is never used to select compose file, project, host, secrets, image or policy; the label does not establish deployment target. | `scripts/deploy.sh:7–10,12–28` | Explicit fail-closed environment mapping and target assertion. |
| F-1777 | P0 | Deployment pulls/builds mutable artifacts without commit/digest/signature/SBOM/vulnerability assertion. | `scripts/deploy.sh:12–18` | Immutable signed artifact and supply-chain gates. |
| F-1778 | P0 | No preflight verifies required secrets, registry, Docker resources, network, volumes or database compatibility before changing services. | `scripts/deploy.sh:7–28` | Read-only preflight with fail-closed checks. |
| F-1779 | P0 | The script comment advertises migrations/seeds but no migration or seed execution/locking/reconciliation is implemented. | `scripts/deploy.sh:20–22` | Versioned migration/seed plan, lock and reconciliation evidence. |
| F-1780 | P0 | Starting MongoDB/Redis and the application is not wrapped in a backup/data-compatibility/transaction boundary. | `scripts/deploy.sh:20–28` | Backup checkpoint, compatibility gate and recovery evidence. |
| F-1781 | P1 | Fixed ten-second sleep is used instead of bounded readiness polling with component-specific health criteria. | `scripts/deploy.sh:24–25` | Bounded readiness checks for MongoDB, Redis and backend. |
| F-1782 | P0 | No HTTP readiness, contract smoke, security, migration or data-integrity verification occurs before declaring release success. | `scripts/deploy.sh:27–36` | Post-deploy verification gates with fail-closed result. |
| F-1783 | P0 | No rollback trap, previous-version pin, canary, traffic cutover or automatic recovery exists on partial startup/failure. | `scripts/deploy.sh:7–36` | Tested rollback/canary/recovery procedure. |
| F-1784 | P0 | `Deployment complete!` is printed after `docker compose up -d` without confirming service health, readiness or correct version. | `scripts/deploy.sh:27–33` | Success only after verified release criteria. |
| F-1785 | P1 | `docker compose ps` and backend logs are observation only; the script does not parse health, exit codes or error patterns into a gate. | `scripts/deploy.sh:32–36` | Machine-verifiable health/error gate and artifact capture. |
| F-1786 | P1 | No explicit operator authorization, change ticket, audit actor or immutable deployment record is captured. | `scripts/deploy.sh:7–36` | Authorized release record with commit, actor, target and timestamp. |
| F-1787 | P1 | No resource cleanup/rollback exists for failed pulls, builds, partial starts or orphaned services. | `scripts/deploy.sh:12–36` | Trap-based cleanup and failure recovery tests. |
| F-1788 | P1 | No database/Redis version, schema, index or backward/forward compatibility check is performed. | `scripts/deploy.sh:20–28` | Compatibility and index/migration gates. |
| F-1789 | P1 | No backup restoration test or documented recovery point/object is tied to the deployment operation. | `scripts/deploy.sh:20–28` | Verified backup/restore drill and deployment linkage. |
| F-1790 | P2 | Operational output includes decorative emoji and generic status text rather than structured, machine-readable deployment evidence. | `scripts/deploy.sh:10,13,17,21,24,30,36` | Structured release report and evidence artifacts. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |

| F-1794 | P0 | Medical claims/advice have no citation, evidence date, clinical reviewer identity, approval/version or disclaimer. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:16–17,27–28,38–39` | Evidence-backed medical content, review metadata and disclaimer. |
| F-1795 | P0 | Diabetes article contains prevalence and screening assertions without visible jurisdiction/source/date validation. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:35–39` | Sourced, current, jurisdiction-aware clinical claims. |
| F-1796 | P1 | English title exists but English excerpt/body are absent, creating incomplete locale parity and potentially inconsistent SEO representation. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:13–17,24–28,35–39` | Complete reviewed locale fields or explicit unavailable-state contract. |
| F-1797 | P1 | No image/media, canonical URL, structured data, author profile, category hierarchy or internal-link metadata is seeded for SEO/article truth. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:11–45` | UI-matching canonical/JSON-LD/media/author/category contract. |
| F-1798 | P1 | Fixed starter catalog has no source, content version, effective date, expiry or retirement/410 lifecycle. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:11–45,51–55` | Content provenance and lifecycle with stale-content handling. |
| F-1799 | P1 | Upsert by slug lacks visible unique-index verification, collision policy, version conflict or reconciliation of expected IDs/content. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:50–55` | Unique slug/index and deterministic reconciliation. |
| F-1800 | P1 | Reruns overwrite `published_at` and `is_deleted`, potentially reviving/de-dating article records without review or audit. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:51–55` | Immutable publication timestamps and reviewed update semantics. |
| F-1801 | P1 | No moderation, plagiarism/quality, language, safety or clinical-content validation is performed before publication. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:11–55` | Automated and human content safety/quality gates. |
| F-1802 | P1 | No article-to-service/source-of-truth link guarantees CTA claims such as available home labs or consultations are actually live and serviceable. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:17,28,39` | Live serviceability/source verification for CTAs. |
| F-1803 | P1 | No explicit indexing/noindex, sitemap, IndexNow, canonical or 404/410 lifecycle is coupled to publication state. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:47–55` | Publication-indexing lifecycle and URL-status tests. |
| F-1804 | P1 | Mongo disconnect occurs only on success; catch logs and exits without guaranteed cleanup or structured operational result. | `scripts/seed-articles.ts:47–61` | Finally-based cleanup and structured failure/audit reporting. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |

| F-1812 | P0 | `requires_prescription` flags are hardcoded without prescription validation, controlled-drug policy, contraindication or dispensing enforcement. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:28,45,62,79` | Prescription/dispensing policy and enforcement tests. |
| F-1813 | P1 | No stock, availability, pharmacy/seller, batch, lot, expiry, recall or reservation fields/source are represented. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:18–87` | Inventory/seller/lot/expiry/recall source of truth. |
| F-1814 | P1 | Hardcoded `usage_count` values are presented without event provenance or aggregation integrity. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:32,49,66,83` | Event-derived metrics with provenance. |
| F-1815 | P1 | Fixed string IDs and `insertMany` have no unique-index assertion, deterministic reconciliation, version conflict or audit actor. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:20,37,54,71,89` | Canonical IDs, unique indexes, reconciliation and audit. |
| F-1816 | P0 | Collection-wide deletion and insertion are not coupled to a transaction/backup/rollback boundary. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:14–16,89–96` | Transactional/backup-aware import with recovery. |
| F-1817 | P1 | No localization completeness, source review or locale-specific clinical copy contract is represented. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:20–87` | Reviewed multilingual medicine content or explicit unavailable state. |
| F-1818 | P1 | No medication safety metadata such as dosage, contraindications, interactions, warnings or age/pregnancy restrictions is represented. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:18–87` | Safety metadata source and pharmacist review. |
| F-1819 | P1 | Errors are logged but do not set an explicit nonzero process result, while success logging claims four medicines without post-write validation. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:89–95,99–100` | Fail-closed exit and post-seed reconciliation. |
| F-1820 | P2 | The script relies on untyped JavaScript and inline catalog objects for a regulated/financial-adjacent source. | `scripts/seed-medicines.js:1,18–87` | Strict schema/types and CI/static checks. |

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |

| F-1821 | P0 | Apply mode requires storage/database credentials but no approved operator, release, target bucket/account or Mongo database identity gate. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:32–42` | Authorized target-bound release gate and immutable audit record. |

| F-1825 | P0 | S3 object upload and Mongo logo-link update are not atomic; partial failure can create orphan objects or DB links to unavailable assets. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:43–46` | Transactional/outbox/compensating publication with reconciliation. |
| F-1826 | P0 | Mongo `updateOne` by company code does not assert unique insurer existence, expected current state or matched/modified result. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:42–46` | Unique target assertion and fail-closed update result. |
| F-1827 | P1 | Logo link update lacks operator/source-review/version/approval provenance and does not preserve an immutable asset history. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:43–49` | Asset publication provenance and history. |
| F-1828 | P1 | No cache invalidation, public URL reachability, accessibility metadata or CDN/object availability verification occurs after upload. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:43–49` | Post-publish URL/cache/accessibility checks. |
| F-1829 | P1 | No orphan-object, superseded-logo or deletion lifecycle is defined despite public immutable cache headers. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:43–46` | Asset lifecycle and orphan reconciliation. |
| F-1830 | P1 | No idempotency/replay or release version protects repeated apply runs from silently replacing approved assets and timestamps. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:18,43–49` | Deterministic release key and replay-safe publication. |
| F-1831 | P1 | `S3_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is trusted as a concatenated public URL without origin/HTTPS/tenant/path validation. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:22,44–46` | Validated allowed origin and URL contract. |
| F-1832 | P1 | Asset source URLs are copied from the manifest without revalidation or legal/licensing evidence at publication time. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:43–46` | Source/licensing provenance and review record. |
| F-1833 | P1 | S3 client lifecycle and network retry/timeouts are not explicitly managed; partial network failures are not compensated. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:39–51` | Bounded retries/timeouts and failure reconciliation. |
| F-1834 | P2 | Type assertions and inline `require`/`any` reduce compile-time assurance for a public asset publication path. | `scripts/publish-insurance-logo-assets.ts:21,33,35,39–40,51` | Strict types and static checks. |

## Insurance catalog reconciliation findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1835 | P0 | Runtime manifest validation checks only that `companies` is an array; code/name/URL/hash completeness, uniqueness, signature and version are not validated. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:18–35` | Strict signed manifest schema and reconciliation validation. |
| F-1836 | P0 | Apply mode requires only `MONGO_URI` and has no database/environment identity, target collection, operator or approval gate. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:28–53` | Fail-closed target/authorization gate. |
| F-1837 | P1 | Lowercasing codes without validating duplicates can collapse distinct manifest entries onto one target; no unique-index assertion is performed. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:56–75` | Canonical normalized code and unique-index checks. |
| F-1838 | P1 | Sequential upserts are not transactionally protected; mid-loop failure can leave a partial insurance catalog with no rollback/reconciliation. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:56–79` | Import transaction/lock, recovery and post-run reconciliation. |
| F-1839 | P1 | Existing records are not checked for source/provenance/version/status conflicts; `$setOnInsert` silently treats stale or conflicting records as valid existing data. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:56–75` | Conflict detection and reviewed reconciliation. |
| F-1840 | P1 | New records receive fixed `catalog_version: 1` and basename-only provenance, without immutable manifest digest, source review or operator/change identity. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:60–72` | Content-addressed version/provenance and audit. |
| F-1841 | P1 | Inserted records are marked pending/inactive, but no subsequent review/approval/readiness workflow is coupled to this script. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:67–70` | Explicit reviewer queue and activation gate. |
| F-1842 | P1 | Regulatory source URLs are copied without URL/HTTPS/domain/source-date or verification evidence validation. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:18–25,60–66` | Validated regulatory source and evidence metadata. |
| F-1843 | P1 | Script does not reconcile expected manifest entities against database duplicates, orphans, missing records, field drift and inactive/pending counts after import. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:76–79` | Deterministic completeness/drift reconciliation. |
| F-1844 | P1 | No backup, recovery point, import lock or change-ticket linkage is tied to apply mode. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:38–80` | Backup/lock/recovery and change record. |
| F-1845 | P1 | No explicit idempotency/replay key or manifest digest prevents repeated apply runs from being ambiguous across catalog revisions. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:28,56–75` | Manifest-digest idempotency and revision semantics. |
| F-1846 | P1 | No source/asset approval, logo status/hash/URL reconciliation is performed despite the manifest containing logo fields. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:18–25,39–46` | Complete logo/source reconciliation or explicit separation. |
| F-1847 | P1 | Success output reports inserted/existing counts without proving database target, unique records, expected fields or post-write integrity. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:76–80` | Fail-closed machine-readable integrity report. |
| F-1848 | P2 | Catalog reconciliation depends on a basename-only provenance label and local file path rather than a durable content-addressed source record. | `scripts/reconcile-insurance-catalog.ts:30,65–67` | Durable source digest, URI and review provenance. |

## Catalog governance backfill findings added during Phase 0B

| ID | Severity | Finding | Direct evidence | Required acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F-1849 | P0 | Apply/rollback require only `MONGO_URL` and a confirmation string; no environment/database identity, operator authorization, approved window or target collection assertion is present. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:119–143` | Fail-closed target and authorized migration gate. |
| F-1850 | P0 | Runtime policy filters are generic `Record<string, unknown>` and do not validate schema, source freshness, jurisdiction, revocation/expiry or reviewer authority of inherited verification. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:18–46,54–56` | Typed collection-specific policy and verification evidence. |
| F-1851 | P0 | Medicine/provider records can inherit public eligibility from legacy flags without proving current regulatory/license validity, review date or revocation status. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:24–38,76–85` | Current verified source and expiry/revocation gate. |
| F-1852 | P1 | `governanceMissing` requires all three fields to be absent and does not distinguish null, malformed, stale, conflicting or manually reviewed values. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:48–56` | Valid-state schema and conflict-aware migration. |
| F-1853 | P1 | Dry-run reports counts only; it does not reconcile expected IDs, duplicates, field drift, source freshness, or the records that will be changed. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:58–70` | Deterministic dry-run manifest and fail-closed reconciliation. |
| F-1854 | P0 | Apply uses broad collection-level `updateMany` operations without transaction, lock, per-record version guard or backup/recovery boundary. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:73–101` | Transactional/locked migration with recovery and conflict tests. |
| F-1855 | P0 | The second `updateMany(governanceMissing)` is not explicitly constrained to records excluded from the inherited update, creating a risk of unintended overwrite if filters/field state evolve. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:76–97` | Disjoint predicates and post-update invariant proof. |
| F-1856 | P1 | Apply overwrites governance provenance and backfill timestamps without preserving prior values, operator identity, migration digest or change ticket. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:78–95` | Immutable migration audit and prior-value/version capture. |
| F-1857 | P1 | `medical_review_status: 'approved'` is inherited from legacy verification for medicines/providers without evidence of clinical reviewer/approval semantics. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:76–85` | Separate regulatory/clinical/public approval meanings. |
| F-1858 | P1 | `indexing_eligibility` is set false even for public-eligible records, but no downstream sitemap/noindex/indexing reconciliation is coupled to the migration. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:78–84` | Explicit indexing lifecycle and URL-state verification. |
| F-1859 | P1 | Rollback matches fixed provenance/timestamp values rather than a unique migration ID and prior-value snapshot; copied/retained values can make unrelated records eligible for rollback. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:104–115` | Migration-scoped rollback with immutable snapshot. |
| F-1860 | P1 | Rollback unsets governance fields and can erase meaningful pre-existing values if provenance/timestamp fields were later reused or copied. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:107–115` | Non-destructive rollback and prior-value restoration. |
| F-1861 | P1 | No uniqueness/index, orphan, completeness or collection-name existence checks are performed before or after the six-collection migration. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:24–46,127–140` | Collection/index preflight and post-run reconciliation. |
| F-1862 | P1 | No per-collection isolation, checkpoint or resume marker exists; failure after earlier collections leaves an undocumented partial migration. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:127–142` | Checkpointed/resumable migration and partial-failure recovery. |
| F-1863 | P1 | No immutable audit records who applied/rolled back governance, which database/commit was targeted, or the exact matched/modified records. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:135–149` | Structured migration audit artifact. |
| F-1864 | P2 | Script uses fixed string provenance literals and a generic policy abstraction that can drift from collection-specific governance semantics. | `scripts/backfill-catalog-governance.ts:24–46,83–95,107–114` | Versioned typed policy registry and contract tests. |

| F-1869 | P2 | The deprecated file provides no direct link to the replacement's contract, dry-run artifact, approval requirement or operator documentation beyond a free-form message. | `scripts/seed-insurance-companies.ts:4–13` | Actionable migration guidance and evidence link. |
